Its decoder-KV execution path preserves a text-only key--value cache across a session, encodes only new dialogue turns, and evaluates transient candidate-label tokens without adding them to the persistent cache. The same checkpoint also predicts task type, difficulty, reasoning mode, and expected output length, and supports custom zero-shot labels. For task generation, we construct a task ontology with 23 families, 115 task types, 345 routable subtypes, 1,173 synthetic examples, and an orthogonal axis of 30 domains. Using this structure, we generate 150,000 verifier-scored tasks and 15,000 open-ended tasks. We then train the Qwen3 decoder on these tasks, while explicitly separating learned request prediction from per-task policies for attributes such as eligibility, cost, cache reuse, safety, and sovereignty. Across six LiveBench subsets, the router outperforms the mean candidate; on the selected 1,000-task subset, it achieves an aggregate top-1 score of 0.707 versus 0.696 for the strongest fixed model, with benchmark-dependent gains.

</details>


### [233] [Improving Evaluation Realism with Inference-Time Compute and Deployment Scaffolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02302)
*Axel Ahlqvist,Richard Guan,Juan-Pablo Rivera,Adeline Kassler,Dmitrii Troitskii,Alexandra Souly,Kai Fronsdal,Robert Kirk,John Hughes*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了“批判性细化”和“DISH”两种技术，通过增加推理时计算量和模拟真实部署环境，显著提升了AI对齐评估的真实性，使其更难被模型识别为测试场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决“评估感知”这一核心障碍：高性能模型能够识别自己正处于测试而非部署状态，从而削弱安全评估结论的可信度。

Method: 1. 批判性细化（Critique Refinement）：利用额外的推理时计算，生成多个候选动作，并根据目标模型的反馈进行细化，选择最接近部署行为的候选项。2. DISH（Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness）：在编码场景中，通过代理 harness 包装目标模型，缩小模拟环境与真实部署环境的差距。

Result: 在多个目标模型上的测试表明，这两种技术具有组合效应，同时应用时带来的真实性提升优于单独使用任一技术；自动化方法能有效提升评估真实性，且比单纯延长审计时间更高效地利用了额外计算资源。

Conclusion: 自动化方法能够显著改善对齐评估的真实性，且通过优化计算资源分配（而非增加评估时长）能更有效地实现这一目标。

Abstract: A core obstacle to alignment evaluation is evaluation awareness: capable models can tell when they are being tested rather than deployed, weakening the conclusions a safety evaluation can support. We present two techniques that make simulated alignment evaluations harder to distinguish from real deployments. Our first technique, critique refinement, spends additional inference-time compute on each simulator action: the simulator generates multiple candidate actions, refines them using feedback from an instance of the target model on how to make them more realistic, and continues the evaluation with the most deployment-like candidate. Our second technique, DISH (Deployment-Imitating SWE-Agent Harness), wraps the target in an agent harness, reducing the gap between simulated and real deployment environments in coding settings. We test the techniques on multiple target models and find that they compose: applying both yields larger realism gains than either alone. Our results show that automated approaches can improve the realism of alignment evaluations, and that these improvements use additional compute more effectively than making the audits longer.

</details>


### [234] [SALA: Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment for Complex Reasoning in In-Context Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02336)
*Zhao Ji,Wenqing Chen,Zhixuan Chu,Jianxing Yu,Jingping Liu,Shanhe Zhao,Zibin Zheng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 针对复杂推理的上下文学习，传统方法依赖表面相似性，逻辑方法规则僵化，SALA 框架通过自动学习推理操作并结合动态时间规整实现灵活的语义逻辑对齐，在多个基准测试中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的基于表面相似性的检索方法无法捕捉底层问题解决逻辑，而现有的基于逻辑的方法依赖预定义步骤和精确匹配标准，难以处理灵活多样的推理过程。

Method: 提出 SALA 框架，自动学习任务特定的推理操作，将其嵌入连续语义空间，并使用动态时间规整（DTW）对齐推理序列，实现软性、灵活的逻辑匹配。

Result: 在四个推理基准测试和三个大语言模型上的实验表明，SALA 优于现有的演示选择方法。

Conclusion: 操作归纳和逻辑语义对齐在提升复杂推理的上下文学习效果中起关键作用，SALA 提供了兼具灵活性和可解释性的解决方案。

Abstract: Effective in-context learning (ICL) for complex reasoning relies on selecting the right demonstrations. Traditional retrieval methods based on surface similarity fail to capture the underlying problem-solving logic. Recent logic-based methods address this by matching predefined reasoning steps, but the rigid rules and exact-match criteria is improper to handle flexible or diverse reasoning processes. To address the problem, we propose SALA, a Semantic-Aware Logical Alignment framework. Instead of relying on a fixed inventory, SALA automatically learns task-specific reasoning operations. It then embeds these operations into a continuous semantic space and uses dynamic time warping (DTW) to align the reasoning sequences. This approach allows for soft, flexible matching of reasoning logic while remaining highly interpretable. Experiments across four reasoning benchmarks and three LLMs demonstrate that SALA outperforms existing demonstration selection methods. Further analysis confirms the roles of the operation induction and the logical semantic alignment.

</details>


### [235] [Diagnosing with Insights: Structured Analysis of Agent Failures via Behavioral Abstractions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02371)
*Jiayi Bi,Yanjie Gao,Yuanmin Xie,Liqun Li,Tianyin Xu,Fan Yang,Mao Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AGENTSCOPE，一种神经符号方法，用于诊断LLM代理失败模式，通过结构化表示和神经不变量实现精准故障定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM代理的普及，理解与诊断其失败对提升有效性和可信度至关重要。传统软件调试技术难以应对LLM代理失败，而完全依赖LLM判断又不可靠，且人工分析复杂轨迹成本过高。

Method: AGENTSCOPE将代理行为轨迹抽象为结构化表示，引入神经不变量（neural invariants）定义行为属性，结合LLM引导推理对结构化表示进行验证，从而定位失败步骤及类型。

Result: 在公开数据集（Who&When）和自建数据集（AgentErrata）上验证，AGENTSCOPE在故障定位和归因准确率上显著优于当前最先进方法。

Conclusion: 将结构化抽象与LLM引导推理相结合，可实现对代理失败的有效、可靠且可解释的诊断。

Abstract: With the proliferation of LLM agents, the ability to understand and diagnose failures in agents is essential to achieving superior effectiveness and trustworthiness. As agent failures often manifest via long and complex trajectories, manually finding the needles in the haystack is untenable. However, traditional diagnosis techniques for software bugs can hardly address LLM agent failures, while completely relying on LLMs as the judge yields unreliable diagnosis results. To overcome these challenges, this paper presents AGENTSCOPE, a new neuro-symbolic approach for agent failure mode diagnosis. The key principle of AGENTSCOPE is to abstract agent behavior, based on its trajectories, into structured representations. Furthermore, AGENTSCOPE introduces the concept of neural invariants to specify agent behavior properties. AGENTSCOPE leverages LLM-guided reasoning atop the structured representation against neural invariants to pinpoint both the failure step and its type in the trajectory. We show the effectiveness of AGENTSCOPE on publicly available agent failure datasets (Who&When) and a more comprehensive dataset created by us (AgentErrata), where AGENTSCOPE significantly outperforms the current state of the art in fault localization and attribution accuracy. Our work shows that integrating structured abstractions with LLM-guided reasoning enables effective, reliable, and interpretable diagnosis for agent failures.

</details>


### [236] [Contrastive Explanations in Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02399)
*Xiang Yin,Nico Potyka,Antonio Rago,Francesca Toni*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对定量双极论证框架（QBAF）的对比解释方法，用于解释两个主题论证之间的差异，并介绍了基于移除、梯度和 Shapley 值的对比归因函数（CAF），在医疗和偏见识别场景中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的 QBAF 解释方法主要针对单个主题论证的推理结果，缺乏对两个论证之间差异的对比解释能力，限制了其在增强 AI 模型可解释性方面的应用。

Method: 提出对比解释的一般形式，定义对比归因函数（CAF）及其应满足的通用属性，并设计了基于移除、梯度和 Shapley 值的三种具体 CAF 实现方法。

Result: 建立了 CAF 的理论属性，并通过医疗和偏见识别场景的实证研究展示了对比解释在增强可解释性和识别偏差方面的实用性。

Conclusion: 对比解释方法有效扩展了 QBAF 的解释能力，为 AI 分类任务提供了更具差异性和洞察力的可解释性工具，在敏感领域如医疗和公平性分析中具有应用价值。

Abstract: Argumentation frameworks are useful tools for representing and reasoning with information in a variety of settings, e.g. in supplementing AI models as they perform classification tasks, with a notable benefit of providing additional explainability. In this paper, we introduce contrastive explanations for Quantitative Bipolar Argumentation Frameworks (QBAFs), one such formalism. Unlike most existing explanations for QBAFs, which explain the reasoning outcome of a single argument of interest (i.e. a topic argument), contrastive explanations explain the difference between two topic arguments. We introduce a general form of contrastive attribution functions (CAFs) and establish a set of general properties they should satisfy. We introduce CAFs based on removal, gradients and Shapley-values, and study their properties. Finally, to illustrate contrastive explanations, we demonstrate their usefulness in healthcare and bias identification settings.

</details>


### [237] [UTP-Bench: Uncertainty-aware Travel Planning Benchmark](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02421)
*Etcharla Revanth Rao,Priyanshu Karmakar,Shubhojit Mallick,Manish Gupta,Shreya Ghosh,Abhik Jana*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对现有旅行规划基准忽略现实不确定性的问题，提出了UTP-Bench，一个基于印度504个城市真实数据的大规模不确定性感知旅行规划基准，并引入三项新指标评估LLM生成行程在应对延误和人群波动时的鲁棒性，实验显示当前顶尖模型在此方面与人类规划仍有显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的旅行规划基准（如TravelPlanner和TripCraft）假设环境是确定性的，仅评估静态约束满足情况，忽略了交通延误、人群波动等现实世界中频繁发生的不确定性因素对行程可行性的影响，导致无法评估生成计划的鲁棒性。

Method: 构建了UTP-Bench基准数据集，整合了印度504个城市的景点、餐饮、住宿及多模态交通网络真实数据；引入实证延误分布和人群密度模式以模拟现实干扰；提出了三个评估指标：缓冲充足度评分（BAS）、人群感知时间评分（CATS）和交通延误吸收评分（TDAS），用于量化行程在随机条件下的鲁棒性。

Result: 对GPT-5、Qwen3、Mistral和Phi-4等最先进大语言模型的实验表明，模型生成的行程在时间缓冲、延误感知交通调度和人群敏感规划方面，与人类撰写的行程相比存在显著差距。

Conclusion: UTP-Bench填补了不确定性感知旅行规划评估的空白，揭示了当前LLM在处理现实世界随机干扰时的不足，为开发更具鲁棒性的自动化旅行规划系统提供了重要的基准和评估方向。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently demonstrated strong capabilities in automated travel itinerary generation. However, real- world travel planning is inherently uncertain: transportation delays, crowd fluctuations, and unexpected stochastic delays frequently inval- idate otherwise feasible schedules. Existing benchmarks like TravelPlanner and TripCraft assume deterministic environments, evaluating only static constraint satisfaction and ignoring whether generated plans remain robust when such uncertainties arise. To address this limitation, we introduce UTP-Bench1 , a large-scale benchmark for uncertainty-aware travel planning. The dataset integrates real-world travel data spanning 504 cities of India, including attractions, restau- rants, accommodations, and multi-modal trans- portation networks. To model realistic disrup- tions, UTP-Bench incorporates empirical delay distributions and crowd-density patterns col- lected from major cities, enabling evaluation of travel plans under stochastic conditions. We further propose three evaluation metrics, namely Buffer Adequacy Score (BAS), Crowd- Aware Timing Score (CATS), and Transport Delay Absorption Score (TDAS), which quan- tify the ability of generated itineraries to main- tain robustness against transit delays and crowd variability. Experiments with state-of-the-art LLMs like GPT-5, Qwen3, Mistral and Phi-4 re- veal substantial gaps between model-generated and human-authored plans, particularly in tem- poral buffering, delay-aware transportation scheduling, and crowd-sensitive planning.

</details>


### [238] [CivBench: A Long-Horizon Benchmark for Tool-Mediated Agents in Civilization VI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02459)
*Austin Tudor David Andrews,Liam Wilkinson,Jamie Heagerty,Harry Coppock,Jakob Nicolaus Foerster,Rui Ponte Costa*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CivBench，一个基于模型上下文协议（MCP）的开源基准测试，用于评估语言模型代理在长视野、工具中介环境中的表现。研究发现，尽管有工具访问和明确指导，代理在主动监控战略状态和执行计划承诺方面存在显著偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估语言模型代理在复杂、长视野任务中的表现，特别是需要持续规划、状态监控和工具调用的环境。现有基准测试难以捕捉代理在长期任务中的行为特征和偏差。

Method: 通过CivBench环境，使用76个MCP工具和叙述层将视觉游戏状态转换为结构化文本。在23次可接受的运行中，对四个模型家族的代理行为进行表征，引入主动监控率（PMR）和RAG@10两个接口级指标来衡量代理行为。

Result: 代理在战略状态监控方面表现不足，查询频率低于指导要求（每30-75次而非每20次），且在20次可检测的失败中有7次未在游戏结束前20次内查询。代理执行计划承诺的能力也有限，RAG@10在48.2%到65.8%之间。

Conclusion: 代理在长期任务中存在系统性偏差，表现为对可用战略状态的监控不足和对自身计划承诺的执行不力。这些偏差源于指令执行问题而非能力缺失，为改进代理设计提供了方向。

Abstract: We present CivBench, an open-source benchmark for evaluating language model agents in long-horizon, tool-mediated environments through the Model Context Protocol (MCP). A single episode spans 300+ turns and produces thousands of tool calls over a large action space, requiring sustained planning, state monitoring, and execution under partial observability. The environment exposes 76 MCP tools and a narration layer that converts visual game state into structured text.
  We use CivBench to characterise agent behaviour across four model families in 23 admissible runs. The sample is a pilot, not a model ranking: aggregate outcomes do not reliably discriminate models at this scale. Instead, we introduce two interface-level metrics that the environment makes measurable: Proactive Monitoring Rate (PMR), capturing whether agents actively query latent strategic state, and RAG@10, capturing whether commitments stated in structured planning reflections are executed within ten subsequent turns.
  Across runs we observe two consistent patterns under a shared playbook protocol. Agents under-monitor strategically relevant state that is available but requires explicit querying: despite playbook guidance to query victory progress every 20 turns, agents do so only every 30 to 75 turns, and in 7 of 20 detectable defeats they failed to query within the 20 turn warning window before game end. Agents also frequently fail to execute near-term commitments stated in their own planning reflections (RAG@10 between 48.2% and 65.8% across models). Both patterns arise despite tool access and explicit guidance, and we interpret them as deviations under instruction rather than absences of capability.
  We release the environment, scenarios, logs, metrics, and analysis pipeline at https://github.com/lmwilki/civ6-mcp

</details>


### [239] [Collective creativity in hybrid societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02620)
*Mason Youngblood,Katie Mudd,Manuel Anglada-Tort,Cameron Jones,Elena Miu,Diana Omigie,Margaret Schedel*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 生成式 AI 对创造力的影响取决于人机混合群体的构成，而非单纯看 AI 是否有帮助或有害。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究人员对生成式 AI 是丰富还是贫化文化存在分歧，这种分歧源于混淆了创造力的两个不同组成部分：新颖性（单个作品的属性）和多样性（群体的属性）。

Method: 提出将创造力理解为“混合集体”（人机交互群体）的属性，分析 AI 辅助构思对个体新颖性和群体多样性的影响，并研究不同代理构成比例和连接方式对结果的决定作用。

Result: AI 辅助构思能可靠地提高个体输出的新颖性，但会缩小整体的多样性；然而，通过优化人机混合群体的构成（代理类型、比例和连接方式），混合群体可以超越单一人类或单一机器群体，实现个体增益积累而不侵蚀集体多样性。

Conclusion: 关键问题不再是 AI 是否帮助或损害创造力，而是哪些混合组合能让个体增益积累而不侵蚀集体多样性。

Abstract: Generative AI is changing how cultural artifacts are created and circulated, and with it our understanding of creativity itself. Researchers disagree about whether these tools enrich or impoverish culture, and we argue that much of that disagreement comes from conflating two distinct components of creativity: novelty, a property of single artifacts, and diversity, a property of populations. We argue further that creativity in the context of generative AI is best understood as a property of hybrid collectives, or populations of interacting people and algorithms, rather than of individuals. AI-assisted ideation reliably raises the novelty of individual output while narrowing diversity in the aggregate, but this is not an inevitable consequence of putting machines in the loop. Because humans and models search in complementary ways, mixed groups can outperform and out-diversify groups of either kind alone, and machine-discovered solutions can enter human culture and persist there. What decides the outcome is composition: which agents are present, in what proportion, and how they are connected. The question is no longer whether AI helps or harms creativity, but which mixtures let individual gains accumulate without eroding collective diversity.

</details>


### [240] [Loom: Weaving Diagnostic Strands into Free-Text Consensus via Embedding-Space Reweighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02649)
*Ron Begleiter,Katya Egert Berg,Gilad Saban,Gil Shabat*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Loom是一个用于根因分析的生成式共识框架，通过模块化启发式方法生成假设，利用连续嵌入空间和迭代质心重加权算法解决冲突，仅需单次LLM调用即可实现高效准确的共识生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在工业环境中部署NLP系统时，聚合噪声和冲突的文本假设是一个基本挑战。单体LLM代理虽然表达性强，但存在上下文限制、幻觉累积和推理延迟高的问题；传统弱监督方法虽然统计严谨，但仅限于离散类别。

Method: Loom通过模块化启发式方法（动态填充特定实体、时间和指标的诊断模板）生成开放形式的假设，将其投影到连续嵌入空间，并使用基于迭代质心的重加权算法解决冲突信号，最终共识权重用于指导单次轻量级LLM合成步骤。

Result: 在OpenRCA基准测试中，Loom在Bank和Market-2数据集上达到最先进自主代理的准确率，在Market-1和Telecom数据集上略低，但在所有四个数据集上每个事件仅需一次LLM调用，速度提升约26倍（使用8B参数合成器时约33倍）。

Conclusion: Loom在准确性和效率之间取得了良好平衡，部署经验表明确定性共识有助于建立领域专家的信任，同时揭示了代理深度与推理延迟之间的权衡以及冗余检测的负面结果。

Abstract: Aggregating noisy, conflicting textual hypotheses into a reliable consensus is a fundamental challenge when deploying NLP systems in real-world industrial settings. While monolithic Large Language Model (LLM) agents offer unbounded expressivity for tasks like Root Cause Analysis (RCA), they suffer from context limits, compounding hallucinations, and prohibitive inference latency. Traditional weak supervision offers statistical rigor but is mathematically restricted to discrete classes. We present Loom, a generative consensus framework deployed for real-world RCA that bridges these paradigms. Loom aggregates open-form hypotheses emitted by modular heuristics (diagnostic templates dynamically populated with episode-specific entities, times, and metrics) by projecting them into a continuous embedding space, and resolves conflicting signals with an iterative centroid-based reweighting algorithm. The resulting consensus weights ground a single lightweight LLM synthesis step. Evaluated on the OpenRCA benchmark, Loom occupies the accuracy--efficiency Pareto frontier: it matches a state-of-the-art autonomous agent on Bank and Market-2 and trails on Market-1 and Telecom, while using a single LLM call per incident on all four datasets ($\sim$26$\times$ faster; $\sim$33$\times$ with an 8B-parameter synthesizer). We discuss our deployment experience, highlighting lessons learned regarding the trade-offs between agentic depth and inference latency, negative results in redundancy detection, and how deterministic consensus fosters trust among Subject Matter Experts~(SMEs).

</details>


### [241] [Door-in-the-Face Requests and Refusal Behaviour in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02707)
*Til Jordan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究"门面效应"（Door-in-the-Face）技术是否适用于语言模型，发现效果因模型家族而异：Anthropic 模型有效，OpenAI 和 Google 模型则适得其反。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索人类心理学中的说服技巧（如门面效应）是否能迁移到语言模型上，以理解模型对请求序列的反应机制及对齐策略的差异。

Method: 对来自三个提供商的九个生产模型进行测试：先提出大请求被拒后，再提出较小的相关请求，对比直接提出小请求的合规率；设置无关主题拒绝作为控制组；将拒绝请求改写为解释性请求以分析拒绝原因。

Result: Anthropic 的 Opus 5 模型在拒绝大请求后对小请求的合规率从 29.3% 提升至 65.8%；OpenAI、Google 及 Haiku 4.5 模型合规率下降 15.5 至 23.0 个百分点；控制组显示让步效应普遍存在，但模型对刚拒绝行为的反应不同；将拒绝请求改写为解释性请求可消除 263/265 的拒绝。

Conclusion: 人类影响技术向语言模型的迁移具有模型家族特异性；请求内容类型（指令 vs 解释）是决定退让策略是否有效的关键因素。

Abstract: Does the door-in-the-face technique work on language models? In humans, a large request that is refused makes a smaller follow-up request more likely to be granted. We test this on nine production models from three providers: each model refuses a large request, then receives a smaller version of the same request, and we compare its compliance with asking directly. The answer depends on the model. On Anthropic's frontier models the technique works: Opus 5 answers the smaller request 65.8% of the time after refusing the larger one, against 29.3% when asked directly. On the frontier models of OpenAI and Google, and on Haiku 4.5, it backfires, lowering compliance by 15.5 to 23.0 points. A control locates the effect: a refused large request on an unrelated topic does less than the related one on all nine models, so the concession itself matters everywhere, while the reaction to having just refused something differs by model family. The technique does not transfer to refusals drawn from public benchmarks. What decides whether a retreat can work is what the request asks for: rewriting 265 refused requests for usable instructions into requests for explanations of the same topic removed the refusal in 263 cases. Human influence techniques port to language models one model family at a time.

</details>


### [242] [Repo-To-Skill: Distilling GitHub Repositories Into AI4AI Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02749)
*Jianlyu Chen,Yuyang Hu,Hongjin Qian,Jiawei Liu,Wenqing Wei,Xiaolong Chen,Defu Lian,Zhicheng Dou,Chaozhuo Li,Qiwei Ye,Zheng Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DisCo，一个基于技能的自主机器学习研究代理，通过将领域操作知识蒸馏为可复用的技能库（AREX-Skill Library），显著提升了代理在多个基准测试上的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的自主ML研究代理虽然具备规划、执行和验证架构，但缺乏关键的“操作知识”（operational knowledge），即让方法真正落地的具体诀窍。这些知识虽存在于代码库和论文中，但形式不适合机器直接加载使用，导致代理在每次任务中需重新发现，效率低下。

Method: 提出DisCo框架，采用两种互补的蒸馏模式：1) 任务无关型蒸馏，将广泛使用的ML代码库浓缩为通用技能；2) 任务导向型蒸馏，针对具体任务生成特定技能。由此构建了包含5000+已验证技能的AREX-Skill Library，涵盖20个领域和178个能力家族，并在固定GPT-5.5骨干网络和执行预算下进行评估。

Result: 在固定模型骨干和执行预算的前提下，配备技能的代理相比无技能版本性能显著提升：MLE-bench提升134.3%，PaperBench提升34.4%，FrontierCS提升9.2%，PassNet提升14.0%。

Conclusion: 将分散的领域操作知识蒸馏为紧凑、已验证的技能并复用，能有效弥补自主代理的知识缺口，在不增加模型规模或计算预算的情况下，大幅提高端到端ML研究的效率和成功率。

Abstract: Autonomous agents are beginning to carry out machine-learning (ML) research end to end. These agents combine a model backbone with a harness for planning, execution, memory, and verification, but this architecture still leaves domain-specific know-how outside the agent. We call this missing layer operational knowledge, the know-how that separates knowing a method from making it work. That knowledge is not absent from the field. It appears in repositories and papers, but in forms written for human readers and too large to load during a task. Once distilled into compact, verified skills, this knowledge can be reused across tasks rather than rediscovered during each run.
  We present DisCo, a skill-powered research agent that creates skills and uses them during research. Its distillation runs in two complementary forms: task-agnostic, condensing the field's widely used repositories into reusable skills, and task-oriented, producing the skills a concrete task calls for. The former, applied across the open ecosystem, yields the AREX-Skill Library, with 5,000+ verified skills distilled from 1,000 widely used ML repositories and organized into 20 areas and 178 capability families. With the GPT-5.5 backbone, research harness, and downstream execution budget held fixed, the skill-equipped research agent scores 134.3% higher on MLE-bench, 34.4% higher on PaperBench, 9.2% higher on FrontierCS, and 14.0% higher on PassNet than the same agent without skills. These gains come from adding distilled operating context under that fixed setup.

</details>


### [243] [Bilevel Coordinated Reflection: A Game-Theoretic Approach to Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02750)
*Yihang Chen,Yuxiang Chen,Yuxuan Huang,Meng Fang,Weilin Luo,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于双层协调博弈和随机反思记忆上升（SRMA）的多智能体LLM系统理论框架，通过环境接地评估提升任务解决率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多智能体LLM系统缺乏对协调、记忆改进和外部验证作用的统一理论解释，需要建立更严谨的数学模型来分析和优化系统性能。

Method: 将协调器-工作者交互建模为双层协调博弈，分析反思作为语义记忆状态的随机移动，提出SRMA算法，仅在接地评估风险严格降低时接受候选记忆。

Result: 证明了SRMA在特定条件下精确收敛，提供了置信度门控和分段平稳环境的重新锚定保证，在500个SWE-bench实例上达到72.2%的解决率。

Conclusion: 环境接地评估对于提升多智能体LLM系统性能至关重要，SRMA框架提供了理论保证和实证支持，显著优于现有基准方法。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems commonly use an orchestrator to decompose a task for a team of workers and then improve through textual reflection. Despite strong empirical results, these systems lack a unified account of coordination, memory improvement, and the role of external verification. We model orchestrator-worker interaction as a bilevel coordination game: under bounded coupling, the workers' local-update game is an approximate potential game whose equilibrium slack is controlled by decomposition quality. We then analyse reflection as stochastic movement over semantic memory states. For free-form reflection, we derive a finite-time upper bound, prove worst-case tightness, and give a positive lower bound under a falsifiable persistent-harm condition. We further prove an information-theoretic impossibility result: no gate that observes only the generated transcript can improve uniformly over text-indistinguishable environments, whereas an environment-grounded gate can. Motivated by this separation, we introduce Stochastic Reflective Memory Ascent (SRMA), which accepts a candidate memory only after a grounded evaluation risk strictly decreases. Under calibration and non-degenerate corrective mass, SRMA converges exactly, geometrically or polynomially; matching constructions show that both rate regimes are order-tight. We also provide confidence gating for stochastic evaluation and re-anchoring guarantees for piecewise-stationary environments. Experiments instantiate these objects with environment-grounded metrics and test the predicted coordination and drift laws. On 500 SWE-bench instances, the complete Kimi-based system resolves 72.2% versus a 70.8% public mini-SWE-agent reference. Code: https://github.com/YihangChen9/Bilevel-Coordinated-Reflection

</details>


### [244] [Measurement-Driven Sub-Network Selection for On-Premise Retrieval-Augmented Factory Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02760)
*Vasileios Rizeakos,Georgios Paisios,Alexandros Machairas,Michael Birbas,Athanasios Bachoumis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对工厂边缘设备的结构化压缩与检索增强适应方法，证明了模型大小不再是预测答案质量的可靠指标，并通过共享权重的超网和就地蒸馏技术，实现了在有限内存和功耗下，为不同设备选择最优子网络，成功在制造业手册案例中恢复了大部分性能损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大型语言模型因体积过大而无法部署在工厂车间硬件上的问题，同时确保在资源受限的边缘设备上仍能提供高质量的机器文档问答服务。

Method: 采用结构化压缩和检索增强适应（retrieval-grounded adaptation）技术；将部署视为适应后的选择问题，在通用能力下限和内存预算约束下，基于 judged answer quality 和 on-device throughput 为每个设备选择一个子网络；使用三明治式就地蒸馏（sandwich-style in-place distillation）训练权重共享的超网以降低选择成本。

Result: 在制造业手册案例中，提取过程导致 judged quality 损失 13.7%，但检索增强蒸馏恢复了其中三分之二的损失，最终性能仅比未剪枝模型低 4.6%；该助手可在三个异构边缘层级上运行，待机功耗仅为 1.3 至 5 瓦。

Conclusion: 经过结构压缩和检索增强适应后，模型大小不再能可靠预测答案质量；通过优化大小、速度或质量的单一规则都会牺牲其他指标，而基于多维度约束的子网络选择策略能实现最佳平衡，使高性能助手能高效部署于异构边缘设备。

Abstract: On-premise assistants can give factory workers conversational access to machine documentation, but models capable of the task rarely fit shop-floor hardware. We show that after structural compression and retrieval-grounded adaptation, model size is no longer a reliable predictor of adapted answer quality: general capability falls almost linearly with parameter count, while judged retrieval-augmented answer quality does not. We therefore treat deployment as a post-adaptation selection problem, committing one sub-network per device on judged answer quality and measured on-device throughput under a configurable general-capability floor and memory budget; rules that optimize size, speed, or quality alone each give up capability or throughput. A weight-shared supernetwork trained with sandwich-style in-place distillation keeps this selection inexpensive. In a manufacturing-manual case study, extraction costs 13.7 percent of the unpruned model's judged quality and retrieval-grounded distillation returns it to within 4.6 percent, recovering two thirds of the loss, and the same assistant runs across three heterogeneous edge tiers at 1.3 to 5 watts standby.

</details>


### [245] [SafeEvolve: Harness-Policy Co-Evolution from Agent Experience for Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02786)
*Qinghua Mao,Wanying Qu,Dadi Guo,Leitao Yuan,Qingyu Liu,Yu Li,Guanxu Chen,Yanwei Fu,Xi Lin,Xia Hu,Dongrui Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出 SafeEvolve，一个经验驱动的自我进化框架，通过 harness-policy 协同进化提升 LLM 智能体的安全性，在 AgentDojo 上实现 3 倍 ASR 降低且提升良性效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有安全对齐机制依赖外部 harness 更新或策略优化，单独使用无法桥接运行时控制与内在安全性，导致 LLM 智能体在有害最终响应和多步执行轨迹上存在安全风险。

Method: SafeEvolve 利用已完成 on-policy 轨迹的安全经验驱动 harness-policy 协同进化循环：harness 侧将轨迹级安全证据转化为安全提示和分层技能的有界组件级更新；策略侧采用两阶段 SFT-RL 范式，先通过 harness-use SFT 引导策略利用进化后的 harness 构件，再通过 harness-augmented RL 在验证器分解奖励下塑造多步探索中的自主安全行为。

Result: 在智能体安全基准测试中，SafeEvolve 相比现有基线实现更强的安全 - 效用权衡；对 Qwen3.5-4B，在 AgentDojo 上 ASR 降低 3 倍，良性效用从 59.79% 提升至 61.86%。

Conclusion: SafeEvolve 通过 harness-policy 协同进化，将安全经验转化为进化的运行时 harness 和改进的策略行为，有效平衡智能体的安全性与实用性。

Abstract: The performance of LLM-based agents is jointly shaped by the base model and the harness used when interacting with the environment. This exposes them to safety risks in both harmful final responses and multi-step execution trajectories. Existing safety alignment mechanisms often rely on either external harness updates or policy optimization, yet applying either paradigm in isolation fails to bridge runtime control with intrinsic safety. We propose SafeEvolve, an experience-driven self-evolving framework for agent safety alignment. SafeEvolve leverages safety experience from completed on-policy trajectories to drive a continual loop of harness-policy co-evolution. On the harness side, SafeEvolve converts trajectory-level safety evidence into bounded, component-level updates across safety prompt and hierarchical skills, yielding auditable and reversible harness artifacts. On the policy side, SafeEvolve follows a two-stage SFT-RL paradigm, where harness-use SFT bootstraps the policy to actively leverage evolved harness artifacts, and harness-augmented RL further shapes autonomous safety behaviors during multi-step exploration via verifier-decomposed rewards. Through harness-policy co-evolution, SafeEvolve converts safety experience into an evolved runtime harness and improved policy behavior. Experiments on agentic safety benchmarks show that SafeEvolve achieves a stronger safety-utility tradeoff than existing baselines. For Qwen3.5-4B, SafeEvolve achieves a $3\times$ ASR reduction on AgentDojo while improving benign utility from 59.79% to 61.86%.

</details>


### [246] [Large Language Models (LLMs) for Telecom Root Cause Analysis (RCA): A Structured Reasoning Framework for Evidence-Grounded Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02805)
*Hao Zhou,Mandar Kulkarni,Hao Chen,Yan Xin,Charlie,Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对5G/6G网络根因分析（RCA）中直接应用大语言模型（LLM）导致的幻觉和推理不稳定问题，提出了一种结构化推理框架。该框架通过将异构遥测数据规范化、强制执行决策路径推理以及生成基于证据的解释，显著提升了诊断准确性和决策一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代5G及新兴6G网络具有复杂的跨层依赖关系，使得性能降级的根因分析极具挑战性。虽然LLM具备强大的推理能力，但直接应用会导致幻觉、推理不稳定以及与结构化网络证据对齐不佳的问题，亟需一种能结合领域知识和结构化证据的新方法。

Method: 提出了一种面向电信RCA的LLM结构化推理框架：首先将异构网络遥测数据组织为规范上下文；其次在诊断过程中强制执行决策路径推理；最后生成基于证据的解释以实现可靠的故障识别。

Result: 在TeleLogs和TelecomTS两个5G RCA数据集上的实验表明，该框架相比基线技术一致地提高了诊断准确性和决策一致性。

Conclusion: 结构化推理设计对于构建下一代电信网络中实用的基于LLM的根因分析系统至关重要，能有效解决幻觉问题并提升系统可靠性。

Abstract: Root cause analysis (RCA) is a critical task in telecom network operations, but diagnosing performance degradations in modern 5G and emerging 6G networks remains challenging due to complex cross-layer dependencies. While large language models (LLMs) offer promising capabilities for reasoning and knowledge integration, directly applying vanilla LLMs to telecom RCA often leads to hallucination, unstable reasoning, and poor alignment with structured network evidence. This work first reviews the evolution of telecom RCA from rule-based and machine learning (ML) approaches to emerging LLM-enabled techniques, and provides an overview of recent paradigms, including structured reasoning, retrieval-augmented knowledge grounding, agentic orchestration, and verifiable reasoning. Building upon these insights, we propose a structured reasoning framework for LLM-enabled telecom RCA that aligns diagnostic reasoning with telecom-specific evidence and domain knowledge. The proposed approach first organizes heterogeneous network telemetry into canonical contexts, and then enforces decision-path reasoning during diagnosis, and finally generates evidence-grounded explanations for reliable fault identification. Experimental results on two 5G RCA datasets, TeleLogs and TelecomTS, demonstrate that the proposed framework consistently improves diagnostic accuracy and decision consistency compared with baseline techniques. These cross-dataset results highlight the importance of structured reasoning design for practical LLM-based RCA systems in next-generation telecom networks.

</details>


### [247] [AI Contextual Measurement for Recovering Individual and Group-Level Effects: Validation Against Survey Measures and an Occupational Application](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02821)
*Wenxin Jiang,Xuyang Wang,Yuxiao Wu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出AICOME框架，利用AI构建个体层面的社会特征指标，并验证其在情境模型中恢复组间和组内效应的能力，发现该框架在数据丰富时能有效复现调查变量中的关键信息，但在信息受限时表现下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统调查常缺失某些社会、组织和职业特征的测量，研究者日益借助AI填补这一空白，但现有方法多将AI测量视为单纯的响应预测，缺乏对个体与群体层面效应恢复能力的系统评估框架。

Method: 提出AICOME（AI COntextual MEasurement）框架，将AI生成的个体层面指标分解为群体聚合值和个体偏离值，从而同时估计组间和组内关联；使用2022年中国家庭追踪调查（CFPS）数据，以职业为分组结构，针对计算机使用、外语使用、周工作时长和管理职责等变量，进行响应层面、模型层面、情境分析及边界条件验证。

Result: AI情境测量在拥有丰富个体与职业特征数据时，能有效恢复观测调查变量中包含的情境模型信息；周工作时长验证效果最强，AI指标成功复现了CFPS中与满意度相关的显著负向组间和组内关联；但当仅依赖职业和基本人口统计信息，或多个相关概念同时缺失时，模型性能明显下降。

Conclusion: AICOME框架适用于从丰富现有数据集中恢复少量理论关键构念，为AI驱动的社会科学测量提供了系统评估方法，但其有效性高度依赖于可用信息的丰富程度和构念的独立性。

Abstract: Researchers increasingly use artificial intelligence to construct measures of social, organizational, and occupational characteristics that are absent from conventional surveys. We propose AICOME, AI COntextual MEasurement, a framework for evaluating whether AI-derived respondent-level measures can recover individual and group-level effects in contextual models. The key idea is that an AI measure constructed at the respondent level can be used to derive its group-level aggregate and its individual deviation, allowing researchers to estimate both between-group and within-group associations rather than treating AI measurement as response prediction alone.
  We validate the framework using the 2022 China Family Panel Studies (CFPS), where occupations provide the empirical grouping structure and several job-related survey variables provide validation benchmarks. For computer use, foreign-language use, weekly hours, and management responsibilities, we compare survey measures with AI-derived measures in response-level, model-level, contextual, and boundary-condition validations. The results show that AI contextual measurement can recover much of the contextual-model information contained in observed survey variables when rich respondent and job characteristics are available. Weekly hours provides the strongest validation case, with AI-derived measures reproducing the large negative between- and within-occupation associations with satisfaction observed in CFPS. The framework also identifies clear boundary conditions: performance deteriorates when information is restricted to occupation and basic demographics, and recovery is weaker when several related concepts are treated as simultaneously unobserved. The findings suggest that AICOME is most useful for recovering a limited number of theoretically important constructs from rich existing datasets.

</details>


### [248] [Discriminative World Models for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02885)
*Kelvin Li,Dhruv Pendharkar,Anish Pahilajani,Chuyi Shang,Leon Oks,Leonid Karlinsky,Rogerio Feris,Trevor Darrell,Roei Herzig*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“预测状态匹配”（predicted-state matching）训练目标，解决现有网页代理世界模型与下游排序器目标不一致的问题，通过在分支网页代理数据集上训练，使预测状态能区分真实结果与替代动作结果，显著提升了动作排序准确率和端到端任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网页代理使用的世界模型通常通过监督式下一状态预测进行训练，生成固定的HTML或AXTree快照表示。然而，该训练目标与下游排序器（Ranker）或过程奖励模型（PRM）的需求不一致，因为排序器依赖预测状态在不同候选动作间具有判别性，而传统的监督训练未能优化这一特性。

Method: 引入“预测状态匹配”作为新的训练目标，要求模型预测的表示能够区分真实产生的状态与由替代动作产生的状态。利用源自WebArena Go-Browse轨迹的分支网页代理数据集进行训练，该数据集在每个决策点包含多个替代动作及其对应的结果状态。

Result: 在预留的预测状态匹配基准测试中，该方法优于传统的监督式下一状态预测世界模型；在WebPRMBench上，该方法提升了PRM风格的动作排序性能，优于仅基于动作的PRM及结合监督式世界模型的PRM；在WebArena-Lite上，使用该世界模型进行测试时动作选择，提高了端到端任务成功率。

Conclusion: “预测状态匹配”训练目标有效对齐了世界模型与下游排序器的需求，通过增强预测状态的判别性，显著提升了网页代理在动作排序和任务执行方面的性能，为构建更高效的测试时动作选择机制提供了新范式。

Abstract: Recent web agents use world models for test-time action selection by sampling candidate actions, predicting the resulting web states, and ranking them with a ranker model or a Process Reward Model (PRM). These world models are typically trained via supervised next-state prediction to generate fixed representations like HTML or AXTree snapshots. However, this objective is misaligned with the downstream ranker, which relies on predicted states being discriminative across candidates to accurately score them. To address this, we introduce predicted-state matching, a training objective where the predicted representation must distinguish the true resulting state from those reached by alternative actions. We train these models using a branching web-agent dataset derived from WebArena Go-Browse trajectories, where every decision point contains multiple alternative actions and their resulting states. Experiments on our held-out predicted-state matching benchmark show that our approach outperforms world models trained with supervised next-state prediction. We further show that our approach improves PRM-style action ranking on WebPRMBench compared with action-only PRMs and PRMs augmented with supervised-next-state world models. Finally, on WebArena-Lite, using our world model for test-time action selection improves end-to-end task success. Our project page is available at: https://dhruvpendharkar.github.io/dwm/.

</details>


<div id='eess.IV'></div>

# eess.IV [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [Data-Efficient Networks for Multi-Contrast MRI Reconstruction based on a Generalized Content/Style Prior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01959)
*Chinmay Rao,Efe Ilıcak,Matthias J. P. van Osch,Mariya Doneva,Laurens Beljaards,Navid Jabarimani,Nicola Pezzotti,Marius Staring*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 CoSMo-RecNet 的模块化框架，用于在低数据条件下学习多对比度 MR 扫描的引导重建模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的端到端引导重建模型需要大量配对的多对比度原始数据进行训练，这限制了它们在低数据环境中的应用。

Method: 提出 CoSMo-RecNet 框架，核心是基于内容/风格模型的可重用多对比度表示，可从大规模公开的非配对多对比度图像数据集中学习，无需 k 空间数据。使用该冻结模型作为多对比度先验，结合参考对比度，将重建问题简化为可通过轻量级展开网络解决的精炼问题。

Result: 在低场 0.3 T M4Raw 数据集上，CoSMo-RecNet 在减少原始训练数据预算时表现出稳定的重建质量。使用 5 个或更少训练受试者时，其重建质量高于在 100 个受试者上训练的参数量匹配的 MoDL。在数据有限且严重分布外的超低场 47 mT Halbach 扫描仪数据集上，CoSMo-RecNet 优于其他可行策略。

Conclusion: CoSMo-RecNet 框架有效解决了低数据条件下多对比度 MR 重建问题，通过利用大规模非配对图像数据学习先验，显著降低了对配对原始数据的依赖，在多种场景下均表现出优越性能。

Abstract: Multi-contrast MR scans contain redundant structural information that can be leveraged during reconstruction and potentially accelerate acquisition times. This idea has inspired end-to-end guided reconstruction models, leveraging one or more contrasts to guide the reconstruction of a different contrast. However, these models require large paired multi-contrast raw datasets for training, limiting their application in low-data regimes. In this work, we propose a modular framework, namely CoSMo-RecNet, for learning guided reconstruction models in the low-data regime. At its core is a reusable multi-contrast representation based on a content/style model, which can be learned from large-scale, publicly accessible, unpaired multi-contrast image datasets, without available k-space data. Using this frozen model as a multi-contrast prior and using a set of reference contrasts, the reconstruction problem reduces to a much simpler refinement problem that can be solved by a lightweight unrolled network and thus learned from small, task-specific reconstruction datasets. We demonstrate the efficacy of CoSMo-RecNet by evaluating it on the low-field 0.3 T M4Raw dataset, showing stable reconstruction quality on decreasing the raw training data budget. CoSMo-RecNet achieved higher reconstruction quality with 5 training subjects or lower compared to a parameter-count-matched MoDL trained on 100 subjects. On a data-limited and severely out-of-distribution ultra-low-field 47 mT Halbach scanner dataset, CoSMo-RecNet was superior to other viable strategies, including classical reconstruction, transfer learning, and zero-shot reconstruction.

</details>


### [250] [Perceptually Regularized Diffusion Model for Image Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02016)
*Chuxiangbo Wang,Pavithra Venkatachalapathy,Ying Liang,Min Wang,Jing Qin,Yifei Lou,Weihong Guo*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种感知正则化的扩散框架，用于图像超分辨率，通过引入感知损失正则化来改善训练收敛并恢复有意义的图像特征，在基准数据集上取得了更好的感知质量和竞争性失真指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统基于模型的方法依赖固定假设且计算密集，深度学习方法中的扩散模型虽感知质量好，但标准扩散训练目标未显式强制感知保真度，导致过度平滑和图像结构丢失。

Method: 提出感知正则化扩散框架，通过基于感知损失的正则化引入先验知识，改进扩散模型的训练目标。

Result: 在基准数据集上的实验表明，该方法在感知质量上有所提升，同时保持了竞争性的失真指标。

Conclusion: 正则化对于基于扩散的超分辨率是有效的，能够改善训练收敛并鼓励恢复有意义的图像特征。

Abstract: Image super-resolution, which aims to reconstruct high-resolution images from their low-resolution observations, is fundamental to medical imaging, remote sensing, surveillance, microscopy, and scientific visualization. Traditional model-based methods formulate super-resolution as an inverse problem with hand-crafted regularization priors. While interpretable and theoretically grounded, they rely on fixed assumptions and require computationally intensive iterative solvers. Deep learning methods offer data-driven flexibility by learning nonlinear mappings from low- to high-resolution images, among which diffusion models have achieved particularly impressive perceptual quality. However, the standard diffusion training objective is a pixel-domain noise-prediction loss that does not explicitly enforce perceptual fidelity, which can lead to oversmoothing and loss of fine image structure. To address these limitations, we propose a perceptually regularized diffusion framework that incorporates prior knowledge through perceptual-loss-based regularization, improving training convergence and encouraging the recovery of meaningful image features. Experiments on benchmark datasets demonstrate improved perceptual quality and competitive distortion metrics, highlighting the effectiveness of regularization for diffusion-based super resolution.

</details>


### [251] [Seeing Beyond the Lesion: Disease Recognition from Reactive CNS Tissue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02390)
*Jan Schnorrenberg,Jan Ernsting,Enrico Küllenberg,Tim Hahn,Benjamin Risse,Christian Thomas*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 本文评估了四种病理基础模型在颅内活检组织切片上的诊断能力，发现即使在没有病变的反应性脑实质中，模型仍能提取微弱疾病信号，但需注意采样偏差（如组织大小）可能带来的虚假预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 颅内活检常因采样误差仅获取反应性、非病变脑组织，导致疾病无法确诊。需评估现有病理基础模型是否能从这类“非诊断性”组织中提取有效疾病信号，并排除采样偏差对模型性能的干扰。

Method: 使用 245 张全切片图像（186 名患者），将四种病理基础模型（UNI2-h, Virchow2, Prov-GigaPath, H-optimus-0）作为冻结的块编码器，嵌入共享的基于注意力的多实例学习框架。通过仅用切片大小的基线模型评估采样偏差影响，并在控制该混淆因素后进行置换检验验证疾病信号的可预测性。

Result: 1）仅凭切片大小即可复现粗略疾病类别预测，表明存在采样偏差；2）控制该偏差后，疾病标签仍可显著预测（p≤10⁻⁴）；3）四种基础模型性能无显著差异，说明当前块表示已足以恢复微弱形态学信号；4）实例贡献图显示预测证据定位于反应性实质而非采样引入的血液等偏差。

Conclusion: 计算病理学基准测试必须包含仅用来源信息的基线以审计采集捷径；在排除该混淆因素后，弱监督模型仍能从传统认为无诊断价值的组织中提取疾病信号，为颅内活检的辅助诊断提供新可能。

Abstract: Sampling error yields exclusively reactive, non-lesional brain parenchyma in a significant proportion of intracranial biopsies, leaving the underlying disease undiagnosed. We benchmark four pathology foundation models (UNI2-h, Virchow2, Prov-GigaPath, H-optimus-0) as frozen patch encoders within a shared attention-based multiple-instance learning framework using 245 whole-slide images from 186 patients with confirmed downstream diagnoses. We first show that coarse disease-category prediction can be reproduced largely from slide size alone. After restricting classification to three finer diagnostic distinctions within common tissue categories, this confound no longer explains performance, yet disease labels remain predictable above chance under permutation testing (p $\le 10^{-4}$ throughout). Surprisingly, performance is statistically indistinguishable across all foundation-model encoders, suggesting that recovering these weak morphological signatures is not limited by current patch representations. Signed instance-contribution maps and expert review further test whether predictive evidence localizes to reactive parenchyma rather than sampling-induced bias like blood introduced during tissue sampling. These results position acquisition-shortcut auditing via a provenance-only baseline as a necessary control in computational-pathology benchmarks, and show, once that confound is removed, that weakly supervised models still recover disease signal from tissue conventionally regarded as non-diagnostic.

</details>


### [252] [LoFi RADIO: A Distilled In-Domain Backbone Applied for Artifact-Severity Grading of Ultra-Low-Field Neonatal Brain MR](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02676)
*Jonathan B. Martin,Yashwant Kurmi,Charlotte R. Sappo*

Main category: eess.IV

TL;DR: 该论文针对超低场MRI新生儿脑成像中的图像伪影质量问题，提出通过路由基础模型教师或蒸馏为单一ViT-S学生模型（LoFi RADIO）来改进多标签伪影严重程度分级，其中蒸馏方法在性能匹配的同时更利于部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超低场MRI（ULF MRI）虽适用于资源匮乏环境下的新生儿脑成像，但存在信噪比低、无屏蔽、扫描时间长等问题，易产生采集伪影，因此需要自动化质量控制。

Method: 1. 评估多种骨干网络与分类MLP的组合；2. 通过每个伪影的门控路由互补的基础模型教师；3. 在未标记的低场MRI数据集上将教师模型蒸馏为单一领域内ViT-S学生模型（LoFi RADIO）。

Result: 路由基础模型教师和模型蒸馏两种策略均提升了加权综合性能；蒸馏后的骨干网络性能匹配或超过门控方法，且无需在推理时部署多个大型基础模型。

Conclusion: 蒸馏方法（LoFi RADIO）在保持高性能的同时降低了部署复杂度，是超低场MRI图像伪影质量控制的有效解决方案。

Abstract: Ultra-low-field MRI makes neonatal brain imaging deploy- able in low-resource settings, but its low SNR, lack of shielding, and long scan duration make it especially prone to acquisition artifacts, motivating automated quality control. We address the LISA 2026 Task 1a challenge: multi-label severity grading (0/1/2) of seven common image artifacts on ULF T2 weighted volumes. We identify that a number of backbones may be successfully paired with a classification MLP, but that no single backbone is uniformly best across artifacts. To improve performance, we evaluate routing complementary foundation model teachers through a per-artifact gate, as well as distilling the teachers into a single in-domain ViT-S student (LoFi RADIO) over an unlabeled low-field MRI corpus. Both of these strategies improve the weighted composite. The distilled backbone matches or exceeds the gate and has the added advantage of not requiring deployment of multiple large foundation models at infer- ence.

</details>


<div id='stat.ML'></div>

# stat.ML [[Back]](#toc)

### [253] [Pooling and Drift in Delayed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01761)
*Melika Baghi*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了延迟反馈下的多臂赌博机问题，提出基于状态通道的学习方法，通过有效维度而非动作数量来衡量学习成本，显著降低了遗憾值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在推荐系统等实际应用中，系统往往需要在得知动作效果之前就采取行动，存在显著的反馈延迟。传统方法认为动作空间越大学习成本越高，但本文指出如果结果仅通过状态依赖于动作，则学习成本应由产生的不同状态数量决定，而非动作总数。

Method: 引入有效维度$v_t$（介于1和状态数量之间）来衡量状态空间的复杂度，分析了旋转算法和实践中使用的单副本算法的理论界限，并探讨了合并相似状态以降低学习成本的方法及其带来的显式偏差。

Result: 证明了旋转算法的遗憾界限为$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(d+1)V\log K})$，单副本算法为$\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{V^{-}}+\sqrt{dT})$；即使在已知延迟损失的情况下，任何算法也无法逃避$Ω(\sqrt{dE\min\{1+\log J,T/d\}})$的下界。实验显示在生成数据上状态通道方法比动作级加权减少高达79%的遗憾，在漏斗族数据上比调优后的minimax最优方法减少32%至68%的遗憾。

Conclusion: 通过状态通道建模，学习成本取决于有效状态维度而非动作数量，合并相似状态可进一步降低成本。该方法在延迟反馈场景下显著优于现有方法，为推荐系统等实际应用提供了更高效的解决方案。

Abstract: A system often has to act long before it learns whether the act worked: a recommender sees a click in seconds and a purchase in days. With $K$ actions and a delay of $d$ rounds, the best rate known for this setting is $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(K+d)T})$ over $T$ rounds, so a longer menu is always more expensive to learn from. It need not be: if the outcome depends on the action only through the state it produced, then one late outcome informs every action that could have produced the observed state, and the price is set by how many genuinely different states the actions produce rather than by how many actions there are. We measure this using an effective dimension $v_t$ between $1$ and the number of states, and prove $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{(d+1)V\log K})$ for a rotating algorithm and $\widetilde{O}(\sqrt{V^{-}}+\sqrt{dT})$ for the single-copy algorithm used in practice, for any budget fixed in advance; merging similar states lowers the price further, at an explicit bias. Even when given the exact losses from $d$ rounds ago, no algorithm escapes $Ω(\sqrt{dE\min\{1+\log J,T/d\}})$, where $J$ counts the drifting directions and $E$ bounds how far losses move while the learner waits. On generated data, the state channel cuts regret by up to 79 percent against action-level weighting and, on the funnel family, by 32 to 68 percent against a tuned minimax-optimal method.

</details>


### [254] [Posterior Tempering Explains Variance Inflation in Linear and Generalized Linear Thompson Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01999)
*Prateek Jaiswal,Debdeep Pati,Anirban Bhattacharya,Bani K. Mallick*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文研究了Thompson Sampling (TS) 算法的变体α-TS，用于解决随机广义线性bandit问题。通过引入α-后验分布，无需假设后验分布的近似即可进行regret分析，并在α∝d⁻¹时达到了O(d^(3/2)√T log T)的最佳regret界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的Thompson Sampling分析需要膨胀后验方差以获得接近最优的regret保证，且以往工作通常假设后验分布的可处理近似。本文旨在形式化方差膨胀的思想，并在不依赖后验近似假设的情况下进行regret分析。

Method: 提出α-TS算法，使用分数或α-后验分布代替标准后验分布。识别先验和奖励分布的一般正则性条件，结合线性bandit问题的最新分析进展与贝叶斯统计文献中的一阶和二阶后验集中理论进行证明。

Result: 对于α∝d⁻¹的特定选择，获得了O(d^(3/2)√T log T)的最佳已知regret界，适用于指数族和子高斯奖励分布。提供了依赖于α的下界，表明regret常数取决于αd的乘积，当α∝d⁻¹时regret缩放为Ω(d^(3/2)√T)。

Conclusion: α-TS算法在不假设后验分布近似的情况下实现了最优regret界，揭示了d^(3/2)因子的来源，并通过结合bandit分析与贝叶斯后验集中理论提供了新的证明技术。

Abstract: We study a variant of the Thompson Sampling (TS) algorithm, called $α$-TS, for solving stochastic generalized linear bandit problems. Existing analyses of TS require inflating the posterior variance to derive near-optimal regret guarantees. We formalize the idea of variance inflation by introducing $α$-TS that uses a fractional or $α$-posterior instead of the standard posterior. Our main contribution is to identify general regularity conditions on the prior and reward distributions that enable a regret analysis of $α$-TS without assuming any tractable approximation of the posterior distribution, unlike previous works. For a specific choice of $α\propto d^{-1}$, our general regret bound yields the best known regret bound of $O(d^{3/2}\sqrt{T}\log T)$ for both the exponential and sub-Gaussian families of reward distributions. We further provide an $α$-dependent lower bound showing that the regret constant depends on the product $αd$, and that when $α\propto d^{-1}$ the regret scales as $Ω(d^{3/2}\sqrt{T})$, explaining the origin of the $d^{3/2}$ factor in the upper bound. Our proof technique adapts and combines recent advancements in the analysis of linear bandit problems with first- and second-order posterior concentration theory from the Bayesian statistics literature.

</details>


### [255] [HyperMC: Multi-Fidelity Hyperparameter Tuning for Stochastic Gradient MCMC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02138)
*Ming Tan,Xiyun Jiao*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出HyperMC和Robust HyperMC，一种结合Hyperband资源分配与核Stein差异（KSD）评估的多保真度超参数调优框架，旨在解决随机梯度马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛（SGMCMC）方法中因缺乏Metropolis-Hastings接受率而导致的超参数调优难题，在固定计算预算下实现更优的后验近似与预测校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SGMCMC方法虽能实现可扩展的贝叶斯推断，但其性能高度依赖步长、小批量大小和Leapfrog步数等超参数；由于大多数SGMCMC算法缺乏Metropolis-Hastings接受率，传统基于接受率的调优方法无法直接应用，亟需一种无需接受率、能在有限预算下高效探索连续超参数空间的自动化调优框架。

Method: 提出HyperMC框架：结合Hyperband风格的连续减半（successive-halving）资源分配策略与核Stein差异（KSD）作为评估指标，通过多轮括号运行平衡广泛探索与精确评估；进一步提出Robust HyperMC，采用全局网格初始化结合精英引导的局部 refinement 策略，以降低对随机候选生成和有限预算下噪声评估的敏感性；并在理论层面证明了在适当近似与集中条件下，该方法能以高概率选出近优配置，并推导出成功选择所需的最小计算预算。

Result: 在逻辑回归、概率矩阵分解和贝叶斯神经网络上的实验表明，HyperMC相比MAMBA、网格搜索及启发式基线方法，在后验近似质量或预测校准方面表现更优；Robust HyperMC则展现出更稳定、可复现的调优结果，显著降低随机性带来的波动。

Conclusion: HyperMC及其鲁棒变体为SGMCMC提供了一种高效、理论保障且实用的超参数自动调优方案，有效克服了无接受率机制下的调优困境，在固定预算下实现了性能与稳定性的双重提升，具有广泛的适用性和推广价值。

Abstract: Stochastic gradient Markov chain Monte Carlo (SGMCMC) methods enable scalable Bayesian inference, but their performance depends strongly on hyperparameters such as the step size, mini-batch size, and number of leapfrog steps. Since most SGMCMC algorithms lack a Metropolis-Hastings acceptance rate, standard acceptance-based tuning methods are not directly applicable. We propose HyperMC, a multi-fidelity tuning framework that combines Hyperband-style resource allocation with kernel Stein discrepancy (KSD) evaluation. By running multiple successive-halving brackets, HyperMC balances broad exploration of a continuous hyperparameter space with increasingly accurate evaluation of promising configurations under a fixed computational budget. We further introduce Robust HyperMC, which uses global grid initialization followed by elite-guided local refinement to reduce sensitivity to random candidate generation and noisy finite-budget evaluations. Under suitable approximation and concentration conditions for the estimated KSD, we establish that the successive-halving component selects a near-optimal configuration among the sampled candidates with high probability and derive a sufficient computational budget for successful selection. Experiments on logistic regression, probabilistic matrix factorization, and Bayesian neural networks show that HyperMC improves posterior approximation or predictive calibration relative to MAMBA, grid search, and heuristic baselines, while Robust HyperMC yields more stable and reproducible tuning results.

</details>


### [256] [Schrödinger Bridges on Lie Group Manifolds for Probabilistic Intrinsic Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02196)
*Shizhe Zhang,Mingyang Zhao,Lei Ma*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出在李群流形上直接进行生成建模的方法，利用薛定谔桥框架处理动能动力学，避免了欧几里得表示中的误差，并开发了两种计算实现方式（WKBC和RCCBM），在多个数据集上验证了方法的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接在几何流形上进行生成建模可以避免将非欧几里得数据展平、重复环境投影和欧几里得表示中坐标不一致所引入的误差。薛定谔桥为规定端点分布之间的熵正则化传输提供了概率生成框架。

Method: 研究李群流形上动能动力学的薛定谔桥，状态为X_t = (g_t, xi_t) in G x g，允许端点观测仅约束实际测量的变量。开发了两种计算实现：Wrapped-Kernel Bridge Calibration (WKBC)用于紧阿贝尔群，Reciprocal Conditional-Control Bridge Matching (RCCBM)用于紧非阿贝尔群。使用校准的初始律和一个学习的Doob控制器进行前向生成。

Result: 在多个李群流形数据集上进行了实验验证，包括蛋白质和RNA扭转、SO(3)、U(n)以及使用mdCATH轨迹的蛋白质构象转变路径生成任务。建立了有界-Lipschitz路径度量中的模块化误差界，清晰分离了端点、控制回归、初始化、离散化等相关近似带来的误差。

Conclusion: 提出的方法在多个李群流形数据集上验证了可行性和一致性，源代码已公开。该方法为在几何流形上进行生成建模提供了有效框架，避免了传统欧几里得表示的误差问题。

Abstract: Generative modeling directly on geometric manifolds can avoid errors introduced by flattening non-Euclidean data, repeated ambient projection, and coordinate inconsistency in Euclidean representations. Schrodinger bridges provide a probabilistic generative framework for entropy-regularized transport between prescribed endpoint distributions. We study Schrodinger bridges for kinetic dynamics on Lie group manifolds with state X_t = (g_t, xi_t) in G x g, allowing endpoint observations to constrain only the variables that are actually measured. In particular, the entropy projection determines the conditional law of the unobserved endpoint velocities.
  For the same observed endpoint bridge, we develop two computational realizations: Wrapped-Kernel Bridge Calibration (WKBC) uses an explicit periodized kinetic kernel on compact Abelian groups, whereas Reciprocal Conditional-Control Bridge Matching (RCCBM) handles compact non-Abelian groups through two-sided endpoint calibration and mollified conditional-control matching. The canonical teacher-mixture path law is itself a Markov reciprocal law, so forward generation uses a calibrated initial law and one learned Doob controller. Moreover, we establish a modular error bound in the bounded-Lipschitz path metric that provides a clean separation of errors due to endpoints, control regression, initialization, discretization, and related approximations.
  Experiments on multiple Lie group manifold datasets validate the feasibility and consistency of our proposed method, covering protein and RNA torsions, SO(3), U(n), and the Protein Conformational Transition Pathway Generation task using mdCATH trajectories in a compact reduced representation. The source code is publicly available at https://github.com/cafferyzhang12/Schr-dinger_Bridge_on_LieGroup.

</details>


### [257] [From topology learning to graph generation: A unifying perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02286)
*Xiaowen Dong,Hoi-To Wai,Siheng Chen,Laura Toni,Dorina Thanou*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文综述了图结构学习领域的统一框架，将图拓扑推断和图生成分布学习视为同一生成过程的逆问题，并探讨了二者的联系、优缺点及未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图结构学习研究主要分为两个独立方向：从观测数据推断单个图的拓扑结构，以及从图实例中学习生成分布以采样新图。本文旨在通过统一框架连接这两种范式，促进跨领域整合。

Method: 采用综述方法，将图拓扑学习和图生成建模为共同数据生成过程的逆问题，系统梳理现有方法，分析其相互关系、优势与局限性。

Result: 建立了连接图拓扑推断与图生成的统一理论框架，明确了不同方法间的内在联系，并识别出跨范式整合的研究机会。

Conclusion: 该综述为图结构学习领域提供了更广泛的跨学科视角，桥接了图拓扑学习与图生成，并指出了未来有前景的研究方向。

Abstract: Learning graph structures from data is a fundamental problem that spans a wide range of signal processing and machine learning tasks. While significant effort has been made to tackle the problem, existing research has largely evolved along two parallel directions. The first seeks to infer the topology of an individual graph from observations supported on it, whereas the second seeks to learn a generative distribution from observed graph instances, enabling the sampling of new graphs. This review presents a unified framework that connects these formulations by viewing them as inverse problems of a common generation process for graph data. We review the major methodologies within this framework, highlight their relationships, strengths, and limitations, and identify opportunities for integrating ideas across paradigms. By bridging graph topology learning and graph generation, this review provides a broader cross-disciplinary perspective on the field and outlines promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [258] [A computational approach to maximum likelihood thresholds for colored Gaussian graphical models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02382)
*Roser Homs,Olga Kuznetsova,Bernadette J. Stolz*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合拓扑数据分析（TDA）与采样方法的计算框架，用于确定彩色高斯图模型（CGGMs）的最大似然阈值（MLT），有效克服了传统符号代数方法（如Gröbner基）的计算瓶颈。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高维小样本场景下，普通高斯图模型的最大似然估计量往往不存在。虽然彩色高斯图模型（CGGMs）通过图着色施加对称约束降低了样本需求，但计算其保证估计量存在的最小样本数（即最大似然阈值，MLT）仍面临巨大的计算挑战，尤其是传统符号代数方法效率低下。

Method: 1. 建立统一的理论框架，将无彩色模型的结果扩展至彩色模型，并将MLT计算转化为几何问题：寻找样本协方差矩阵的最小秩，使其投影几乎必然位于充分统计量锥的内部。
2. 引入新的符号算法。
3. 提出一种集成采样与拓扑数据分析（TDA）的计算研究方法，用于探究充分统计量锥的局部几何结构。

Result: 研究证明了TDA在分析CGGMs似然几何结构方面的潜力，表明其能够显著克服传统符号代数方法（特别是Gröbner基计算）中的计算瓶颈。

Conclusion: 通过结合几何表述、符号算法与TDA技术，本文不仅扩展了CGGMs的理论基础，还提供了一种高效的计算途径来解决高维统计模型中的存在性问题，为复杂统计结构的分析开辟了新方向。

Abstract: Gaussian graphical models (GGMs) are essential tools for interpretable structure learning. However, in high-dimensional, small-sample regimes, the available data is often insufficient for the maximum likelihood estimator to exist. Colored Gaussian graphical models (CGGMs) mitigate this limitation by imposing symmetry constraints through graph coloring, which reduces the required sample size. This minimal number of observations needed to guarantee that the estimator exists almost surely is defined as the maximum likelihood threshold (MLT). Here, we address the computation of the MLT for CGGMs by focusing on its geometric formulation: finding the minimum rank of a sample covariance matrix such that its projection lies almost surely within the interior of the cone of sufficient statistics. We establish a unified theoretical framework, extending results from uncolored to colored models and introducing new symbolic algorithms. Furthermore, we present a computational study integrating sampling with topological data analysis (TDA) to investigate the local geometry of the cone of sufficient statistics. Our results demonstrate the potential of TDA to overcome the computational bottlenecks of traditional symbolic algebraic methods, particularly Groebner basis computations, in analyzing the likelihood geometry of CGGMs.

</details>


### [259] [Momentum in large-batch training: Polyak enlarges the critical batch size, Nesterov improves data efficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02728)
*Jia-Nan Wang,Zixun Huang,Kairui Li,Lei Wu*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文在幂律核回归框架下，分析了动量（Momentum）在单次遍历（one-pass）的大批量训练中何时及如何提升性能，推导了不同优化器的临界学习率及风险动态缩放律，并揭示了动量在不同批量大小区间对数据效率和并行性的差异化作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决大规模批量训练中如何平衡并行效率与数据效率的问题，特别是探究动量机制在单次遍历 regime 下对训练稳定性、风险动态及最终泛化性能的具体影响机制。

Method: 采用幂律核回归作为可处理的理论模型，通过定义临界学习率来刻画风险稳定性，推导 SGD、Polyak 动量和 Nesterov 动量的稳定性边界；在此基础上建立全风险动态的缩放律，并在固定数据预算下优化最终步风险，构建批量大小相图。

Result: 1. 获得了三种优化器的临界学习率：$η_{\mathrm{SGD}}^{\mathrm{crit}}\eqsim 1$，$η_{\mathrm{Polyak}}^{\mathrm{crit}}\eqsim \min\{1,B(1-ρ)\}$，$η_{\mathrm{Nesterov}}^{\mathrm{crit}}\eqsim \min\{1,B^β(1-ρ)\}$。
2. 推导了包含早期瞬态、幂律衰减至噪声底板的完整风险动态缩放律。
3. 构建了批量大小三阶段相图：Polyak 动量扩大了保持最佳小批量数据缩放指数的临界批量大小，增强并行性；Nesterov 动量因前瞻机制抑制噪声积累，在大批量区间具有更优的数据效率。
4. 数值实验验证了理论预测的稳定性边界、风险动态及相图。

Conclusion: 动量在大批量训练中的作用依赖于批量大小：Polyak 动量通过扩展临界批量大小在不牺牲数据效率的前提下提升并行能力，而 Nesterov 动量则在超大批量训练中通过抑制噪声积累实现更佳的数据效率，为不同计算资源约束下的优化器选择提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study when and how momentum improves large-batch training in the one-pass regime, using power-law kernel regression as a tractable setting. We first characterize risk stability through the critical learning rate, defined as the largest learning rate for stable training, and obtain $η_{\mathrm{SGD}}^{\mathrm{crit}}\eqsim 1$, $η_{\mathrm{Polyak}}^{\mathrm{crit}}\eqsim \min\{1,B(1-ρ)\}$, and $η_{\mathrm{Nesterov}}^{\mathrm{crit}}\eqsim \min\{1,B^β(1-ρ)\}$, where $B$ is the batch size, $ρ$ is the momentum factor, and $β>1$ is the capacity exponent. Within this admissible region, we derive scaling laws for the full risk dynamics, capturing the progression from an early transient, through power-law decay, to a noise floor. We then minimize the final-step risk over the admissible learning rates and momentum factors under a fixed data budget, yielding a three-regime batch-size phase diagram that reveals how the role of momentum changes with batch size. Notably, Polyak enlarges the critical batch size, the largest batch size preserving the best small-batch data-scaling exponent, thereby enabling greater parallelism without sacrificing data efficiency. In contrast, Nesterov achieves better data efficiency in the large-batch regime because its look-ahead mechanism suppresses noise accumulation. Numerical experiments validate the predicted stability boundaries, risk dynamics, and batch-size phase diagram.

</details>


### [260] [Full-Model Optimality for Tunable Linear Generative Priors in Compressed Sensing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02790)
*Zhaoming Li,Paul Hand*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文建立了可调线性生成先验在压缩感知中的理论，证明在无噪声高斯压缩感知中，全维度线性先验能获得最小期望重建误差，与去噪任务中的偏差 - 方差权衡不同，表明神经网络先验在压缩感知中的实验优势源于非线性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 之前的实验研究表明，通过调节生成先验的复杂度可以在逆问题（如压缩感知）中获得更低的重建误差，但缺乏理论支持，需要建立可调线性生成先验在压缩感知中的理论基础。

Method: 在可调线性生成先验族（通过奇异值分解自然关联）的设定下，对无噪声高斯压缩感知进行理论分析，证明全维度线性先验的期望重建误差最优性。

Result: 在无噪声高斯压缩感知中，全维度线性先验在整个线性先验族中获得最小期望重建误差，降低复杂度并不能改善期望重建误差，这与去噪任务中的偏差 - 方差权衡行为相反。

Conclusion: 压缩感知中使用神经网络先验的实验优势源于生成模型的非线性，而非简单的复杂度调节，为理解生成先验在逆问题中的作用提供了理论依据。

Abstract: Generative models have been studied experimentally and theoretically as priors for inverse problems such as compressed sensing. Recent work by Gunn et al. studied the use of generative priors with tunable complexity, where a family of generative priors with varying complexity is maintained and a specific complexity can be selected at inversion time. They demonstrated that lower reconstruction errors can be experimentally attained for a variety of inverse problems by appropriately tuning the complexity of the generative prior. In the present paper, we establish theory for compressed sensing in the setting of a tunable family of linear generative priors naturally related through their singular value decompositions. We prove that in noiseless Gaussian compressed sensing, the full-dimensional linear prior attains the minimum expected reconstruction error over the entire family of linear priors. Thus, in this idealized linear noiseless setting, tuning to a lower-complexity prior does not improve the expected reconstruction error. This result is in contract to the behavior of denoising, where lower complexity priors attain lower reconstruction errors due to a standard bias-variance tradeoff. This result indicates that the experimental benefits of tunability in compressed sensing with neural network priors arises due to nonlinearities in the generative models.

</details>


### [261] [Copula Transformations for Data-Consistent Inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02832)
*Troy Butler,Tianyi Jiang,João Silva,Harri Hakula,Timothy Wildey*

Main category: stat.ML

TL;DR: 本文通过 Copula 理论建立了迭代数据一致性反演 (iDCI) 与原始联合 DCI 解之间的关系，证明了精确的 Copula 变换可恢复原始 DCI 解，并提出了自适应参考测度细化策略以提高计算精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: iDCI 避免了高维联合密度的直接近似，但其与原始联合 DCI 解的关系尚不明确，需要理论上的澄清和数学上的建立。

Method: 利用 Sklar 定理将 DCI 更新分解为边缘和依赖变换，通过 Copula 理论表征 iDCI 收敛后的剩余差异，并证明精确 Copula 变换可恢复原始 DCI 解，同时建立近似 Copula 变换的收敛性结果。

Result: 证明了 iDCI 与 DCI 的理论联系，提出了 Copula 变换的 iDCI 解决方案，展示了数量兴趣映射几何对 Copula 变换重要性的影响，并验证了自适应参考测度细化策略的有效性。

Conclusion: Copula 理论为理解 iDCI 与 DCI 的关系提供了数学框架，精确的 Copula 变换可恢复原始 DCI 解，且该方法适用于异构、异步获取实验的广义随机反演问题的逐步细化。

Abstract: Data-consistent inversion (DCI) constructs probability measures whose push-forward distributions agree with observed data, while iterative data-consistent inversion (iDCI) extends this framework to generalized stochastic inverse problems by enforcing multiple push-forward constraints sequentially. Although iDCI avoids the direct approximation of high-dimensional joint densities, its relationship to the original joint DCI solution has remained unclear. In this work, we establish this relationship through copula theory. Using Sklar's theorem, we derive a factorization of the DCI update into separate marginal and dependence transformations and show that the discrepancy remaining after convergence of the iDCI algorithm is entirely characterized by the copulas associated with the observed and predicted joint distributions. This characterization motivates a copula-transformed iDCI solution, and we prove that an exact copula transformation recovers the original DCI solution. We further establish convergence results for approximate copula transformations under converging sequences of reference measures and progressively enriched feasible sets. Numerical examples demonstrate how the geometry induced by the quantity-of-interest map governs the importance of the copula transformation, illustrate an adaptive reference-measure refinement strategy for improving computational accuracy under a fixed sampling budget, and demonstrate the progressive refinement of generalized stochastic inverse problems through heterogeneous, asynchronously acquired experiments.

</details>


<div id='cs.DS'></div>

# cs.DS [[Back]](#toc)

### [262] [Forbidden Subgraphs of Graphs with Low Bandwidth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.01949)
*Maria Chudnovsky,Daniel Lokshtanov,Eran Nevo*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了带宽问题在一般图上是 FPT-可逼近的，给出了一个算法，解决了 Chung 和 Seymour 提出的开放问题，并提供了有界带宽图的禁止子图刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 带宽计算是 NP-hard 问题，即使在受限树类上也没有多项式时间算法或常数因子近似。Chung 和 Seymour 在 1989 年提出开放问题：图的带宽是否可以用其子树的最大带宽来上界。

Method: 设计了一个 FPT-近似算法，输入图 G 和整数 k，运行时间为 $2^{O(9^k)}n^{O(1)}$，输出 G 的子树 T（满足 $bw(T) \geq k$）或带宽不超过 $(10^{85} k^{28})^{4^k}$ 的布局。

Result: 算法成功解决了 Chung-Seymour 开放问题，证明了带宽可以用子树带宽上界，并建立了有界带宽图的禁止子图刻画，类似于树宽的网格小定理。

Conclusion: 该研究为带宽问题提供了 FPT-可逼近性结果，建立了与树宽、路径宽和树深度类似的禁止子结构理论，是图论和参数复杂性领域的重要进展。

Abstract: A layout of a graph G is an injective function $f : V(G) \rightarrow Z$, and the bandwidth of a layout f is $bw(G,f) = max_{uv \in E(G)} |f(u) - f(v)|$. The bandwidth bw(G) of G is the minimum bandwidth of a layout of G. Computing the bandwidth of a graph is a notoriously hard problem: assuming P != NP, there is no polynomial time algorithm, even on very restricted classes of trees [Monien, SIAM Journal on Algebraic Discrete Methods, 1986], and no constant factor approximation, even on trees [Dubey et al., JCSS 2011]. Assuming the Exponential Time Hypothesis, there is no algorithm with running time $f(k)n^{o(k)}$ to determine whether an input graph has bandwidth at most k, even on very restricted classes of trees [Dregi and Lokshtanov, ICALP 2014].
  In this paper we show that {\sc Bandwidth} on general graphs is FPT-approximable. In particular we give an algorithm that takes as input a graph G and an integer k, runs in time $2^{O(9^k)}n^{O(1)}$, and outputs a subtree T of G such that $bw(T) \geq k$ or a layout of G of bandwidth at most $(10^{85} k^{28})^{4^k}$. This resolves in the affirmative an open problem of Chung and Seymour [Discrete Mathematics, 1989], who asked whether the bandwidth of every graph G is upper bounded in terms of the maximum bandwidth of a subtree of G. Our theorem leads to a forbidden subgraph characterization for graphs of bounded bandwidth, and can be seen as an analog for bandwidth of the classic grid minor theorem for treewidth, the forbidden subtree theorem for pathwidth, and the forbidden subpath theorem for treedepth.

</details>


### [263] [The Exact Online Threshold for the Asymmetric Binary Perceptron](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02124)
*Sunghyeon Jo,Taekyun Lee*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文确定了非对称二元感知机在线版本的精确可行性阈值 $\alpha_{\mathrm{on}}(\kappa)$，提出了一种确定性在线算法，在零边际下将可实现的密度上限从 0.1 提升至 0.32747 以上，并通过布朗运动的一维随机控制问题刻画了该阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决非对称二元感知机的在线版本问题，即在矩阵列顺序到达且符号选择不可撤销的约束下，确定是否存在高效的在线算法能满足所有约束，并量化其相对于离线算法的性能差距。

Method: 通过将问题转化为一维布朗运动的随机控制问题来刻画阈值，利用半线单调性（half-line monotonicity）和最终的短修正块（short final correction block）技术，将单坐标的布朗极限推广至 $M=\Theta(N)$ 个约束的同时可行性。

Result: 确定了精确阈值 $\alpha_{\mathrm{on}}(\kappa)$：当 $M/N < \alpha_{\mathrm{on}}(\kappa)$ 时存在多项式时间确定性在线算法以高概率成功，反之则无。在 $\kappa=0$ 时，证明了 $0.32747 < \alpha_{\mathrm{on}}(0) < 0.36664$，显著超越了此前 $0.1$ 的最佳界限；在 $\kappa \to +\infty$ 时与离线存储容量一阶一致，在 $\kappa \to -\infty$ 时与最佳离线多项式时间保证具有相同的渐近尺度。

Conclusion: 在线算法在特定密度范围内可实现与离线算法相当甚至更优的性能保证，特别是零边际下的密度提升超过三倍，证明了通过随机控制理论分析高维约束可行性的有效性。

Abstract: Let $G\in\mathbb{R}^{M\times N}$ have independent standard Gaussian entries. For a fixed margin $κ\in\mathbb{R}$, the asymmetric binary perceptron asks for $σ\in\{\pm1\}^N$ such that $Gσ/\sqrt{N}\geκ\mathbf{1}_M$. We study the online version of this problem, in which the columns of $G$ arrive sequentially and each sign must be chosen irrevocably before future columns are revealed.
  We determine the exact threshold $α_{\mathrm{on}}(κ)$ for every fixed $κ$: for $M/N\toα$ with $α<α_{\mathrm{on}}(κ)$, there is a deterministic online algorithm, using $O(MN)$ arithmetic operations and polynomial bit complexity, that succeeds with high probability, while for $α>α_{\mathrm{on}}(κ)$, no online algorithm succeeds with high probability. The threshold is characterized by a one-dimensional stochastic control problem for Brownian motion. The main difficulty is to upgrade a single-coordinate Brownian limit to simultaneous feasibility of all $M=Θ(N)$ constraints, which we do with half-line monotonicity and a short final correction block.
  At zero margin, we give a computer-assisted proof that $0.32747<α_{\mathrm{on}}(0)<0.36664$. In particular, every density below $0.32747$ is achievable online by such an algorithm, more than tripling the best density previously proved attainable by any polynomial-time algorithm, online or offline (the previous bound was $α\le0.1$, due to Li, Schramm, and Zhou). As $κ\to+\infty$, the online threshold agrees to first order with the offline storage capacity. As $κ\to-\infty$, it has the same asymptotic scale as the best known offline polynomial-time guarantee, while the storage capacity is larger by a factor of order $κ^2$.

</details>


### [264] [Connectivity Oracles Under Vertex Failures via a Simple and Fast Low-Degree Steiner Forest Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02388)
*Sayan Bhattacharya,Ermiya Farokhnejad,Thatchaphol Saranurak,Haoze Wang*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的低度Steiner森林分解算法，允许分解树包含移除顶点，从而在O(mα(n))时间内实现度数为4的分解，并基于此构建了更高效的顶点失效连通性预言机。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的低度Steiner森林分解算法在时间复杂度和度数界限之间存在权衡：要么时间复杂度较高（O(mn log n)）但度数界限较好（4），要么时间复杂度较低（m^{1+o(1)}）但度数界限较弱（O(log²n)）。作者希望打破这种权衡，获得既高效又具有良好度数界限的分解算法，以改进顶点失效连通性预言机的性能。

Method: 作者提出了一种放松的分解定义，允许森林T包含移除顶点集X中的顶点。基于这一放松，设计了一个非常简单的算法，能够在O(mα(n))时间内计算度数为4的分解。然后利用该分解构建确定性连通性预言机。

Result: 获得了确定性连通性预言机，具有Õ(m)空间、Õ(md_*)预处理时间、Õ(d²)更新时间和O(d)查询时间。与现有预言机相比，在多项式对数因子范围内严格改进了所有已知结果，特别是消除了先前算法中的n^{o(1)}因子。

Conclusion: 通过放松分解定义，可以在几乎线性时间内获得高质量的低度Steiner森林分解，这使得构建更高效的顶点失效连通性预言机成为可能，显著提升了预处理和更新时间的性能。

Abstract: We study the low-degree Steiner forest decomposition. Given a graph $G=(V,E)$ and a terminal set $U\subseteq V$, the standard decomposition returns a set $X\subseteq V$ of size at most $|U|/2$ and a forest $T\subseteq G-X$ of maximum degree $Δ$ such that, for every connected component $C$ of $G-X$, some connected component of $T$ contains all terminals in $U\cap V(C)$. This is the central decomposition behind several connectivity oracles under vertex failures [DP20, LS22, LW24]. The state-of-the-art algorithms either take $O(mn\log n)$ time with degree bound $4$ [DP20], or take $m^{1+o(1)}$ time with the weaker degree bound $O(\log^{2}n)$ [LW24].
  We show that if $T$ is allowed to contain vertices of $X$, then a degree-$4$ decomposition can be computed by a very simple algorithm in $O(mα(n))$ time. Further, we show that this relaxed decomposition is equally useful for constructing connectivity oracles under vertex failures. As a consequence, we obtain a deterministic connectivity oracle under $d$ vertex failures with $\tilde{O}(m)$ space, $\tilde{O}(md_\star)$ preprocessing time ($d_\star$ is an upper bound on the number of failed vertices), $\tilde{O}(d^{2})$ update time, and $O(d)$ query time. Up to polylogarithmic factors, this oracle strictly improves all known oracles; in particular, it removes the $n^{o(1)}$ factors from the preprocessing and update times of [LS22, LW24].

</details>


### [265] [The Price of Almost Navigability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02498)
*Tomer Waizer,Yoav Danieli*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文证明了$(1-\epsilon)$-几乎可导航图边数$O(n/\epsilon)$上界的紧确下界，确立了其构造的最优性，并揭示了几乎可导航性与经典Zarankiewicz问题之间的深刻联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决基于图的搜索结构中可导航性（navigability）的稀疏性极限问题。虽然完全可导航图能保证贪婪搜索成功，但其最坏情况需要$\Omega(n^{3/2})$条边，成本过高。Avi和Musco提出的$(1-\epsilon)$-几乎可导航性放宽了条件，给出了$O(n/\epsilon)$的构造上界，但缺乏对应的下界证明以确认该界是否最优。

Method: 通过建立几乎可导航性与经典极值图论中的Zarankiewicz问题（构造具有受限成对邻域重叠的稠密图）之间的理论联系，将极值图构造转化为几何导航的困难实例。研究主要在对数多项式维度的欧几里得空间及$d=O(\sqrt{n}\log^{3/2} n)$维度下构造硬实例（hard instances）。

Result: 1. 证明了匹配的下界，确认了$O(n/\epsilon)$边数构造的最优性，完整刻画了几乎可导航图可实现的稀疏性。2. 对于大多数$\epsilon$值，硬实例存在于低维欧几里得空间。3. 在特定维度下，构造了数据集证明任何完全可导航图仍需$\Omega(n^{3/2})$条边， sharpening了对普通可导航性的理解。

Conclusion: 该工作不仅确立了几乎可导航图构造的紧确界限，还通过连接图论中的Zarankiewicz问题与几何导航问题，揭示了两种看似不同的图稀疏性概念之间的内在联系，为理解基于图的最近邻搜索结构的理论极限提供了新的视角。

Abstract: Navigability is a fundamental property of graph-based search structures and plays an important role in the analysis of nearest-neighbor algorithms. Informally, a graph is navigable if, from any current point and toward any desired target, there is always an outgoing edge that moves strictly closer to that target. While this property provides a strong guarantee for greedy search, it can be inherently expensive: in the worst case, navigable graphs require $Ω(n^{3/2})$ edges, where $n$ is the size of the dataset.
  Recently, Avi and Musco introduced $(1-ε)$-almost navigability, a natural relaxation in which, from every current point, such a progress-making edge is required for almost all targets, while an $ε$ fraction of targets may fail this condition \cite{avimusco2026almost}. They showed that every dataset admits such a graph with $O(n/ε)$ edges.
  In this work, we prove a matching lower bound, establishing the optimality of their construction and giving a complete characterization of the sparsity achievable by almost-navigable graphs. For most values of $ε$, our hard instances lie in Euclidean spaces of polylogarithmic dimension, across the full worst-case range of $ε$, dimension $d=O(\sqrt{n}\log^{3/2} n)$ suffices. The same construction also sharpens our understanding of ordinary navigability: in the latter dimension, we exhibit datasets for which every navigable graph has $Ω(n^{3/2})$ edges.
  Our proof reveals a surprising connection between almost navigability and the classical Zarankiewicz problem of constructing dense graphs with limited pairwise neighborhood overlap. This connection lets us translate extremal graph constructions into hard geometric instances for navigation, linking two seemingly different notions of graph sparsity.

</details>


### [266] [Finding a Shortest Vector and More in $2^{n/2+o(n)}$ Time using $q$-ary Coset Difference Tree](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02764)
*Minki Hhan*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的随机化算法，用于解决精确最短向量问题（SVP），在时间和空间复杂度上均达到$2^{n/2+o(n)}$，并可将该算法推广至解决特定条件下的精确最近向量问题（CVP）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的格密码算法在解决最短向量问题（SVP）和最近向量问题（CVP）时，时间和空间复杂度较高，需要更高效的算法来降低计算资源消耗，尤其是在高维格中。

Method: 提出一种$q$-进制类比的中点Hessian方法，利用周期性高斯函数在$v/q$处的梯度与最短向量$v$近似成比例的特性，结合Wagner广义生日算法的链式组合过程，在中间格链上计算相关陪集梯度。

Result: 算法在时间和空间复杂度上均达到$2^{n/2+o(n)}$，且其变体可在相同复杂度下解决距离保证$\operatorname{dist}(y,\mathcal L)\le 1.039λ_1(\mathcal L)$的精确最近向量问题（CVP），适用于随机目标和Haar-Siegel测度下的随机格。

Conclusion: 该算法显著提升了SVP和特定CVP实例的求解效率，为格密码分析和相关计算问题提供了新的技术路径，尤其在随机格实例上具有实用价值。

Abstract: This paper presents a new randomized algorithm for solving the exact shortest vector problem. For the $n$-dimensional lattice $\mathcal L$, our algorithm runs in time and space $2^{n/2+o(n)}$.
  Our algorithm can be viewed as a $q$-ary analogue of the midpoint Hessian for an odd prime $q$; more precisely, we use the fact that, for a shortest vector $v$, the gradient (rather than Hessian) of the periodic Gaussian function at $v/q$ is nearly proportional to $v$ (up to sign), even after aggregation over a relatively large random affine coset. We compute the relevant coset gradient along a chain of intermediate lattices using a combinatorial procedure inspired by Wagner's generalized birthday algorithm, yielding the $2^{n/2+o(n)}$ time and space complexity.
  A variant of the algorithm solves the exact closest vector problem on every input $(y,\mathcal L)$ with a distance guarantee $\operatorname{dist}(y,\mathcal L)\le 1.039λ_1(\mathcal L)$ within the same time and space complexity. This guarantee holds for a random target and a random lattice drawn according to the Haar-Siegel measure. Thus, this algorithm solves a closest vector problem on such random instances in time and space $2^{n/2+o(n)}$.

</details>


### [267] [Almost Linear 3-Spanners of Temporal Cliques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02851)
*Julia Baligacs,Davide Bilò,Václav Blažej,Maël Dumas,Anna Zych-Pawlewicz*

Main category: cs.DS

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种简单的递归算法，为时间团（temporal cliques）构建了大小为 $n^{1+o(1)}$ 的时间3-跨度图（3-spanner），显著优于此前 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^{3/2})$ 的上界；同时在生命周期受限为 $L$ 时，将大小优化至 $\mathcal{O}(nL)$，优于之前的指数级上界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般时间图可能不存在稀疏的时间 $\alpha$-跨度图，但时间团已知存在稀疏解。然而，现有的时间团 $\alpha$-跨度图构造上界（如 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^{3/2})$ 或 $\mathcal{O}(2^Ln\log n)$）仍有较大优化空间，且已知时间2-跨度图的下界为 $\Omega(n^2)$，因此探索更优的时间3-跨度图构造及其简化方法具有重要意义。

Method: 提出了一种新的简单递归分解策略：利用 $\mathcal{O}(n)$ 条精心选择的边认证大量源 - 目标对的时间连通性，并仅对剩余的对进行递归处理。基于此分解，设计了两种算法：一种用于一般时间团，另一种用于生命周期 $L$ 有界的时间团。

Result: 1. 对 $n$ 个顶点的时间团，构建了大小为 $n^{1+2/\sqrt{\ln n}} = n^{1+o(1)}$ 的时间3-跨度图，改进了此前 $\widetilde{\mathcal{O}}(n^{3/2})$ 的上界。
2. 当生命周期限制为 $L$ 时，构建了大小为 $\mathcal{O}(nL)$ 的时间3-跨度图，改进了此前 $\mathcal{O}(2^Ln\log n)$ 的上界。
3. 新算法在理论界和实现复杂度上均显著优于现有构造。

Conclusion: 通过新颖的递归分解方法，本文不仅大幅降低了时间团上时间3-跨度图的大小上界，还简化了算法构造过程。结果突显了在时间2-跨度图存在 $\Omega(n^2)$ 下界的背景下，时间3-跨度图可实现近线性大小的理论突破。

Abstract: Temporal graphs model dynamic networks by assigning positive integer time labels to the edges, while information propagates along temporal paths, whose edge labels are traversed in nondecreasing order.
