Conclusion: 预训练模型在潜状态推理中存在结构性限制，需要上下文相关的校正机制来有效保持多组件混合状态，为改进LLM推理方法提供了理论指导。

Abstract: LLMs latent-state reasoning methods replace discrete intermediate tokens with continuous states, such as weighted mixtures of token embeddings, to retain multiple possible reasoning directions rather than committing to one. Yet pretrained language models often fail to preserve these mixtures. We study why through a combination of theoretical analysis and controlled empirical investigations on a variety of models. We identify three independent, distinct sources of failure. First, transformer architectures already distort mixture geometry, and training substantially amplifies this effect. Moreover, the failure can occur even if the model transports mixtures perfectly linearly: the softmax readout and autoregressive feedback form a dynamical system that either amplifies small differences until one component of the mixture dominates or contracts different mixtures until they become indistinguishable. We verify this theoretical prediction empirically: the observed transition between contraction and amplification occurs near the theoretical threshold derived by our analysis, and pretrained-model rollouts lie predominantly on the amplifying side. Finally, we generalize to mixtures of many components and show that exact preservation generally requires context-dependent correction, whose required dimensionality can grow with the number of components.

</details>


### [303] [DynG-Diff: A State-Aware Dynamic Guidance Diffusion Framework for Probabilistic Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02068)
*Zhente Zhang,Zhengwei Ni,Wei Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DynG-Diff，一种用于概率多变量时间序列预测的变量敏感动态引导扩散框架，通过两阶段训练和无条件扩散主干解决现有方法在信息异质性上的不足，显著提升了预测精度和抗噪鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于扩散的多变量时间序列预测方法依赖特定任务的有条件范式，缺乏灵活性，且难以应对变量间显著不同的噪声水平和演化模式（即“信息异质性”）问题。

Method: 1. 采用两阶段分离训练策略，利用无条件扩散主干建模多变量时间序列的联合分布；2. 引入轻量级状态感知策略网络，根据实时噪声状态和单步去噪估计自适应推断变量可靠性，输出动态引导强度矩阵；3. 将该动态权重数学化为观测分布的局部精度，在推理阶段为高置信度变量提供精确引导并过滤异常噪声干扰。

Result: 在真实世界基准测试上的广泛实验表明，DynG-Diff 在概率预测性能上可与最先进的条件扩散模型相媲美，并在严重观测损坏情况下展现出更强的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: DynG-Diff 通过动态引导机制有效解决了多变量时间序列中的信息异质性挑战，实现了高精度且鲁棒的概率预测，代码已开源。

Abstract: Probabilistic multivariate time series (MTS) forecasting is crucial for modeling complex dynamical systems. However, existing diffusion-based methods rely on task-specific conditional paradigms that lack flexibility and struggle with inherent "information heterogeneity"--the significantly varying noise levels and evolutionary patterns across variables. To address this, we propose DynG-Diff, a variable-sensitive dynamic guidance diffusion framework for probabilistic multivariate time-series forecasting: (1) DynG-Diff adopts a two-stage separated training strategy and uses an unconditional diffusion backbone to model the joint distribution of multivariate time series. (2) DynG-Diff introduces a lightweight state-aware policy network that adaptively infers variable reliability from real-time noisy states and one-step denoising estimates, outputting a dynamic guidance strength matrix. (3) DynG-Diff mathematically formulates this dynamic weight as the local precision of the observation distribution, enabling precise guidance for high-confidence variables during inference while filtering out interference from anomalous noise. Extensive experiments on real-world benchmarks demonstrate competitive probabilistic forecasting performance against state-of-the-art conditional diffusion models and improved robustness under severe observation corruption.The implementation code is available at: https://github.com/TT-20011031/DynG-Diff

</details>


### [304] [XMerge: Cross-Axis Selection and Reconstructive Layer Merging for LLM Depth Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02083)
*Jundong Hu,Shekar Ramachandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出XMerge，一种无需微调的Transformer层压缩方法，通过跨轴选择和局部边界重建，在保持架构不变的情况下显著优于现有方法，尤其在激进压缩下表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的深度压缩方法在移除完整Transformer层时会导致质量大幅下降，且损失在不同模型间不可预测，需要一种更稳定高效的压缩方案。

Method: XMerge包含两个组件：跨轴选择（识别低相对幅度和角度隐藏状态变化的块）和局部边界重建（重新拟合相邻存活块以匹配原始双块输出），无需任务标签或端到端微调。

Result: 在7个Llama和Qwen模型上测试，XMerge在最激进压缩（k=4）时于CORE和MMLU基准上均排名前列，避免大幅困惑度增加，且是唯一从未崩溃的算子，校准表现最佳。

Conclusion: XMerge通过局部重建获得主要增益，跨轴融合在选择轴不一致时有帮助，额外构建成本可通过数万次请求后的每令牌解码节省收回，是一种高效稳定的层压缩方案。

Abstract: Removing complete transformer layers preserves a standard serving architecture, but existing depth-compression methods can lose substantial quality, and the loss varies unpredictably across models. We introduce XMerge, a post-training method with two components. Cross-axis selection identifies a block with low relative-magnitude and angular hidden-state change, and local boundary reconstruction re-fits the adjacent surviving block to match the original two-block output. XMerge uses no task labels or end-to-end fine-tuning, and it introduces neither architectural changes nor additional inference-time parameters. Across seven Llama and Qwen backbones (0.5B-8B), five published baselines, and three layer-reduction levels, its advantage over baselines is largest at the most aggressive removal: at k=4 it ranks first on six of seven backbones on CORE (a 22-task aggregate) and, separately, on six of seven on MMLU (five of seven on both at once), while avoiding the large perplexity increases of several competing operators. In a task-level bootstrap, the 95% confidence intervals for the three largest CORE margins exclude zero; the remaining margins are consistent with ties. Across the 14 (model, regime) cells it is also the only evaluated operator that never collapses, ranking top-2 in both zero-shot and in-context regimes; on a first calibration probe (one backbone) it is the best-calibrated operator. Ablations show that local reconstruction provides most of the gain, while cross-axis fusion helps when the two selection axes disagree. The additional construction cost is recovered through per-token decode savings after roughly tens of thousands of requests.

</details>


### [305] [TC-Next: Zero-Shot Multimodal Cyclone Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02085)
*Zhe Wang,Sijie Chen,Yiming Luo,Daehyun Kim,Chien-Yi Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TC-Next，一种利用基础模型大气场和卫星图像的多模态深度学习模型，显著提升了热带气旋路径和强度的短期预报精度，并在跨模型和零样本场景下表现出优越的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决传统基于规则的追踪器（如TempestExtremes）在热带气旋路径和强度预报中误差较大的问题，并探索如何利用多模态数据（大气动力学/热力学场与红外卫星图像）及基础模型的预报场来提升预报性能。

Method: 构建名为TropicalCycloneNext (TC-Next) 的多模态深度学习模型。该模型仅在西太平洋区域的GraphCast预报数据上进行训练，输入特征包括基础模型的大气运动学和热力学场以及GridSat红外卫星图像。研究通过对比实验（相对于TempestExtremes）、跨模型迁移（应用于Pangu-Weather和IFS HRES）以及零样本测试（应用于WeatherNext Cyclones）来验证模型性能，并进行了消融实验以评估多模态输入的贡献。

Result: 在西太平洋测试中，TC-Next相比TempestExtremes将路径误差降低了15%-44%，强度误差降低了3-6倍。该模型无需重新训练即可迁移至Pangu-Weather和IFS HRES预报场并保持优势。在2025年西太平洋季节的零样本测试中，其强度误差在所有提前期内均低于WeatherNext Cyclones的专用追踪器，路径误差相当或更低。消融研究证实多模态输入能有效提升各提前期的路径追踪精度及长提前期的强度预测精度。

Conclusion: TC-Next证明了仅依赖通用大气变量和多模态数据的深度学习模型，在热带气旋预报中具有显著的精度优势和强大的跨模型泛化能力，能够有效利用卫星图像等额外模态信息来优化追踪和强度预测任务。

Abstract: We present TropicalCycloneNext (TC-Next), a multimodal deep learning model that forecasts tropical cyclone track and intensity at $6$-$24$ h leads by leveraging a foundation model's forecast fields of atmospheric kinematic and thermodynamic fields and GridSat infrared satellite imagery. Trained only on GraphCast forecasts over the Western Pacific (WP), yet reliant only on generic atmospheric variables, TC-Next on GraphCast lowers track error by $15$-$44\%$ and intensity error by a factor of $3$-$6$ relative to a conventional, rule-based tracker, TempestExtremes; applied without retraining to the forecast fields of Pangu-Weather and IFS HRES, it stays ahead of TempestExtremes on both. Applied zero-shot to the generic weather fields of WeatherNext Cyclones on the 2025 WP season, TC-Next attains lower intensity error at every lead time, and lower or comparable track error, compared to that model's specialized direct tracker in a deterministic comparison. Our ablation studies show that our multimodal model is able to utilize the additional modality to improve performance in tracking errors at every lead time and in intensity prediction at longer lead times.

</details>


### [306] [Compositional Spectral Prompts for LLM-based Online Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02093)
*Seungyoon Choi,Hyunchul Kim,Jae-Gil Lee,Chanyoung Park*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSPOT，一种基于预训练大语言模型（LLM）的在线时间序列预测框架，通过冻结LLM参数并利用基于频域基的组合谱提示（compositional spectral prompts）实现高效在线适应，解决了现有方法在长期适应和未见模式泛化上的不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的在线时间序列预测（OTSF）研究多依赖基于记忆缓冲区的检索策略来适应非平稳环境，但这类框架在长期适应方面表现不佳，且难以泛化到未见过的模式。

Method: CoSPOT利用预训练LLM作为骨干预测器并保持其参数冻结。该方法将时间序列分解为频率基，根据振幅组合相应的谱基提示（spectral basis prompts），以此向模型提供输入的整体分布信息，从而大幅减少在线阶段需要更新的参数量，并将未见模式表示为已学习基提示的新组合。

Result: 在真实世界数据集上的广泛实验表明，CoSPOT在包括扩展在线阶段和存在显著分布偏移的跨数据集设置等挑战性在线场景中，均表现出优越性和实用性。

Conclusion: CoSPOT通过结合LLM的少样本能力与频域组合提示机制，有效克服了传统OTSF方法的局限性，为处理非平稳和 evolving 的时间序列数据提供了一种高效且泛化能力强的新范式。

Abstract: To address the sequential and evolving nature of time series, the Online Time Series Forecasting (OTSF) task has been extensively studied in multiple domains. Existing research focuses on adapting to non-stationary environments by employing memory buffer-based retrieval strategies. However, we observe that such frameworks struggle with long-term adaptation and fail to generalize to unseen patterns. To this end, we introduce CoSPOT, an LLM-based online time series forecasting framework that leverages a pre-trained LLM as the backbone online forecaster, motivated by its strong few-shot capabilities. For efficient online adaptation, CoSPOT keeps the LLM frozen and employs compositional spectral prompts grounded in frequency-domain bases to guide the model with the overall distribution of the input, thereby substantially reducing the number of parameters updated during the online phase. Specifically, CoSPOT decomposes time series into frequency bases and composes the corresponding spectral basis prompts according to their amplitudes, allowing unseen patterns to be represented as new combinations of learned basis prompts. Our extensive experiments on real-world datasets demonstrate the superiority and practicality of CoSPOT across challenging online scenarios, including extended online phases and cross-dataset settings with substantial distribution shifts. Our code is available at https://github.com/seungyoon-Choi/CoSPOT.

</details>


### [307] [Federated LoRA Adaptation of BiomedCLIP Across Four International Chest X-Ray Cohorts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02101)
*Sanjaya Poudel,Nirajan Kunwor,Manish Dhakal,Debesh Jha,Sunil Kumar Gaire*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文评估了联邦学习（FL）结合低秩自适应（LoRA）在生物医学影像（BiomedCLIP）中的应用，发现基于奇异值分解（SVD）的FlexLoRA聚合策略能显著提升多中心胸部X光分类性能，接近集中式训练效果，且无需共享患者数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决生物医学影像中数据隐私法规限制、机构间扫描仪/协议/算力异构性问题，以及在多模态视觉 - 语言模型成为医疗影像分析核心的背景下，探索如何有效聚合联邦LoRA更新。

Method: 在来自三大洲（美国、越南、西班牙）的四个公共队列上，对BiomedCLIP模型进行联邦参数高效微调（PEFT）基准测试；对比了FlexLoRA（基于SVD的乘积空间聚合）、朴素因子平均、FedProx与FedAvg等策略。

Result: 联邦LoRA适配将四个队列的平均共享类AUC从0.687提升至0.802，优于未适配的零-shot模型；相比单队列独立训练，联邦策略提升了弱队列性能并保留强队列表现，接近集中式参考值（0.812）；FlexLoRA至关重要（朴素平均导致AUC下降0.097），而FedProx未见额外收益。

Conclusion: 生物医学视觉 - 语言模型可通过联邦LoRA在异构、地理分布的机构间协同适配，无需集中数据，其中基于SVD的聚合方法是实现高性能的关键。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) lets institutions train a shared model without exchanging data, and Low-Rank Adaptation (LoRA) makes this practical at scale by communicating only compact low-rank updates. Biomedical imaging is a compelling setting for this combination: patient data are archived behind privacy regulations, and institutions differ widely in scanners, protocols, and compute. Such heterogeneity raises the question of how federated LoRA updates should be aggregated, increasingly pressing as multimodal vision-language models become central to medical image analysis. We benchmark federated Parameter-efficient fine-tuning (PEFT) of BiomedCLIP for chest radiograph classification across four public cohorts on three continents (USA, Vietnam, Spain). Federated LoRA adaptation improves shared-class AUC on all four cohorts over the unadapted BiomedCLIP backbone (mean 0.687 to 0.802), showing that the gains come from federated adaptation rather than from the pretrained model's zero-shot ability. Relative to isolated single-cohort training, federation improves the weaker cohorts while largely preserving the strongest and approaches a centralized reference (0.812) that pools all data. The singular value decomposition (SVD)-based product-space aggregation introduced by FlexLoRA is essential to this gain (naive factor averaging drops mean AUC by 0.097), whereas a drift-correcting optimizer (FedProx) shows no benefit over FedAvg in our single-seed runs, consistent with LoRA's low-rank updates already limiting client drift. Biomedical vision-language models can thus be adapted collaboratively across heterogeneous, geographically distributed institutions without centralizing data.

</details>


### [308] [A Unified Rate-Distortion Perspective on Vector, Product, and Scalar Quantization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02107)
*Xianghong Fang,Wenlong Mou,Yuan Yuan,Dehan Kong,Tim G. J. Rudner*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一的率失真（Rate-Distortion）框架来审视离散视觉标记化，证明了最小化失真（而非最大化码本利用率）是重建保真度的核心目标，并在控制特征统计和编码率的公平条件下，确立了VQ优于PQ和SQ的失真层级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的离散视觉标记化（主要由向量、标量和乘积量化驱动）缺乏一个统一的概念框架来理解量化中的权衡，导致在量化器评估中存在歧义，且难以隔离固定速率约束下的内在量化有效性。

Method: 将量化视为有损压缩，建立率失真视角：通过标记数量和码本大小表征标称固定长度编码率，将量化误差表征为失真。在此框架下，通过理论推导与实证分析，控制潜在特征统计量并强制执行相同编码率，以解决三个核心问题。

Result: 1. 理论上和实证上表明，最小化失真（与STE引起的梯度差异直接相关）而非最大化码本利用率，是实现重建保真度的主要内在目标。2. 确立了内在量化比较的两个关键公平条件：控制潜在特征统计量和强制相同编码率。3. 在这些条件下，恢复了现代视觉标记化中的VQ-PQ-SQ失真层级，并实证显示现代VQ方法实现了最低失真。

Conclusion: 该工作为现代离散视觉标记化提供了基础的率失真重构框架，消除了量化器评估中的模糊性，并提供了一个受控框架，用于在固定速率约束下隔离和评估内在的量化有效性。

Abstract: Discrete visual tokenization, predominantly driven by vector, scalar, and product quantization, lacks a unified conceptual framework for understanding quantization tradeoffs. In this paper, we propose a unified rate--distortion perspective on modern discrete visual tokenization. By viewing quantization as lossy compression, we characterize the nominal fixed-length coding rate through token count and codebook size, and quantization error as the distortion. Within this framework, we resolve three central questions. First, we theoretically and empirically show that minimizing distortion, rather than maximizing codebook utilization, is the primary intrinsic objective for reconstruction fidelity, with a direct connection to the STE-induced gradient discrepancy. Second, we establish two critical fairness conditions for intrinsic quantization comparison: controlling latent feature statistics and enforcing identical coding rates. Third, under these conditions, we recover the VQ--PQ--SQ distortion hierarchy in modern visual tokenization and show empirically that modern VQ methods achieve the lowest distortion. This work provides a foundational rate--distortion reframing of modern discrete visual tokenization, resolves ambiguities in quantizer evaluation, and provides a controlled framework for isolating intrinsic quantization effectiveness under fixed-rate constraints.

</details>


### [309] [A Computational Comparison of Fourier Spectral Differentiation and Spatial Automatic Differentiation in Periodic Physics-Informed Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02110)
*Xilai Liang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在周期性物理空间 PINN 中，对比空间自动微分（AD）与傅里叶谱微分，发现后者训练速度提升 2.90-18.52 倍，GPU 内存减少 68.7%-94.1%，且精度相当，但需均匀结构化网格。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PINN 中使用自动微分计算 PDE 残差中的空间导数时，计算和内存成本较高，尤其涉及多阶或高阶导数时。

Method: 在固定网络表示、时间微分、优化器、采样和训练计划的前提下，对比空间 AD 与傅里叶谱微分；傅里叶方法将网络输出变换到频域，通过谱乘法获取导数并复用系数。

Result: 在五种方程 - 框架设置下，傅里叶微分实现 2.90-18.52 倍训练加速，GPU 内存减少 68.7%-94.1%，最终相对 L2 误差量级相同，无一致精度优势。

Conclusion: 对一维周期性基准问题，傅里叶谱微分在保持相当解误差的同时显著降低训练时间和内存，但需均匀结构化空间网格。

Abstract: Physics-informed neural networks (PINNs) commonly evaluate the spatial derivatives appearing in partial differential equation residuals using automatic differentiation (AD), whose computational and memory costs can become substantial when multiple or high-order derivatives are required. We perform a controlled comparison of spatial AD and Fourier spectral differentiation in periodic physical-space PINNs. Within each paired experiment, the neural representation, temporal differentiation, optimizer, sampling procedure, and training schedule are held fixed, so that the two cases differ only in the spatial differentiation procedure. For the Fourier variant, network outputs are evaluated on a uniform periodic grid and transformed to Fourier space, where spatial derivatives are obtained through spectral multiplication and the same Fourier coefficients are reused across derivative orders. We compare the two procedures in standard PINNs for the Allen--Cahn and Korteweg--de Vries equations and in Causal PINNs for the Allen--Cahn, Korteweg--de Vries, and Kuramoto--Sivashinsky equations. Across these five equation--framework settings, Fourier differentiation yields mean paired end-to-end training speedups ranging from $2.90\times$ to $18.52\times$ and reduces peak allocated graphics processing unit (GPU) memory by $68.7\%$--$94.1\%$. The final relative $L_2$ errors remain of the same order, with neither differentiation procedure showing a consistent accuracy advantage. For the one-dimensional periodic benchmarks considered here, Fourier spectral differentiation therefore provides substantially lower training time and memory usage than spatial AD while retaining comparable solution error, at the cost of requiring a uniform structured spatial grid.

</details>


### [310] [Scalable Bayesian Optimization of Composite Functions for Image-Based Inverse Problems in Materials Characterization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02126)
*Dasol Yoon,Poompol Buathong,Chia-Hao Lee,Yujia Zhang,David A. Muller,Peter I. Frazier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 SBOCF 的新方法，用于从电子显微镜图像中高效估计物理参数，通过利用复合函数结构和中间信息，在有限模拟次数下显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从科学图像中估计物理参数是材料表征中的常见逆问题，通常依赖昂贵的物理模拟。在电子显微镜中，样品厚度和晶体倾角是关键参数，但传统的网格搜索扩展性差，神经网络方法需要大量预训练且难以迁移到新条件。

Method: 提出可扩展的复合函数贝叶斯优化（SBOCF）方法，利用图像匹配目标的已知复合结构和模拟图像中的中间信息。通过将 PACBED 图像表示为补丁级摘要和两个校正项，将建模输出从 24,649 个像素减少到 11 个，同时保留原始像素级目标。

Result: 在 50 次模拟评估预算下，SBOCF 在合成 SrTiO3 基准测试中优于标准贝叶斯优化，厚样品情况下中位最终 SSE 降低高达 290 倍。在实验数据上，SBOCF 产生的参数估计与先前报道的值一致，无需特定任务预训练。使用 SBOCF 估计值进行下游叠层成像重建可恢复清晰的原子图像。

Conclusion: SBOCF 是处理涉及昂贵模拟器和高分辨率结构化输出的逆问题的有前景方法，为电子显微镜参数估计提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Estimating physical parameters from scientific images is a common inverse problem in materials characterization that often relies on expensive physics-based simulations. In electron microscopy, specimen thickness and crystal mistilt are critical parameters that govern how electrons scatter through the sample, and therefore the accuracy of any atomic-scale structure recovered from it. They are commonly inferred by matching experimental position-averaged convergent-beam electron diffraction (PACBED) patterns to simulated ones, but grid searches scale poorly and neural-network methods require extensive pretraining that may not transfer to new conditions. Here, we propose scalable Bayesian optimization of composite functions (SBOCF), a simulation-efficient method that exploits the known composite structure of the image-matching objective and the intermediate information contained in simulated images. By representing PACBED images with patch-level summaries and two correction terms, SBOCF preserves the original pixel-wise objective while reducing the number of modeled outputs from 24,649 to 11. Under a budget of 50 simulator evaluations, SBOCF outperformed standard Bayesian optimization with expected improvement on synthetic SrTiO3 benchmarks with thick and thin specimens, reducing the median final SSE by up to 290x in the thick-sample case. On experimental data, SBOCF produced parameter estimates consistent with previously reported values without task-specific pretraining. For a simulated mistilted specimen, using the SBOCF estimates in a downstream ptychographic reconstruction recovered sharp atoms that were otherwise blurred. These results establish SBOCF as a promising approach for inverse problems involving expensive simulators and high-dimensional structured outputs.

</details>


### [311] [GeoSPRINT: Geometric Redundancy-Aware Step Pruning for Inference in Diffusion Trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02160)
*Arpita Joshi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 GeoSPRINT，一种无需训练的扩散模型加速框架，通过分析去噪轨迹的几何特性（超平面性测试）来构建非均匀采样调度，将更多计算步骤分配给高曲率区域，从而在相同计算预算下显著提升生成质量（FID）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型虽然生成质量高，但推理成本昂贵，因为采样需要大量串行的神经函数评估（NFEs）。现有的加速方法要么依赖固定的步长跳过策略，要么基于局部数值误差调整步长，或者需要额外的训练成本，缺乏一种利用轨迹全局几何信息且无需训练的通用加速方案。

Method: 提出 GeoSPRINT 框架，利用潜空间中的超平面性测试（通过 QR 分解高效实现）检测几何冗余步骤；构建非均匀采样调度，在高曲率轨迹区域分配更多步骤；引入轨迹投影分数（$\alpha_{\mathrm{traj}}$）作为衡量轨迹直线度的残差方差指标，用于诊断整流流（rectified flow）质量。

Result: 在 CIFAR-10、LSUN Church 和 Stable Diffusion v1.5 数据集上，GeoSPRINT 在匹配的 NFE 预算下均优于均匀 DDIM 调度。具体而言，CIFAR-10 上 FID 提升 0.7-1.1，且在 NFE≥30 时超越 DPM-Solver++；LSUN Church 上 52 步时 FID 从 1.48 降至 1.26；Stable Diffusion v1.5 上 FID 最大提升 1.93。

Conclusion: 轨迹几何特性为分配推理步骤提供了有效的全局信号；通过优化采样调度质量，可以在无需重新训练模型的情况下，显著提高扩散模型的采样效率。

Abstract: Diffusion models achieve high sample quality but remain expensive at inference time because sampling requires many sequential neural function evaluations (NFEs). Existing acceleration methods either use fixed step-skipping schedules, adapt step sizes based on local numerical error, or require additional training. We introduce GeoSPRINT (Geometric Step Pruning for Inference in Trajectories), a training-free framework for constructing non-uniform sampling schedules from the geometry of denoising trajectories. GeoSPRINT detects geometrically redundant steps using a hyperplanarity test in latent space, implemented efficiently via QR factorization, and converts the resulting redundancy profile into a sampling schedule that allocates more steps to high-curvature regions of the trajectory. In addition, we introduce the trajectory projection score $α_{\mathrm{traj}}$, a residual-variance metric that quantifies trajectory straightness and serves as a model-free diagnostic for rectified flow quality. Across CIFAR-10 ($32{\times}32$), LSUN Church ($256{\times}256$), and Stable Diffusion v1.5 ($512{\times}512$ latent), GeoSPRINT consistently improves over uniform DDIM (Denoising Diffusion Implicit Models) schedules at matched NFE budgets. On CIFAR-10, GeoSPRINT improves FID (Fréchet Inception Distance) by 0.7-1.1 over DDIM across 49-89 NFEs and surpasses DPM-Solver++ at NFE${\geq}30$ despite using a first-order DDIM solver. On LSUN Church, it reduces FID from 1.48 to 1.26 at 52 steps, and on Stable Diffusion v1.5 it achieves up to 1.93 FID improvement over DDIM. These results show that trajectory geometry provides a useful global signal for allocating inference steps and that schedule quality can substantially improve diffusion sampling efficiency without retraining.

</details>


### [312] [DMRL: Document-Mediated Reinforcement Learning for Skill Optimization in Advertising Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02170)
*Wei Zhang,Hongji Li,Song Sun,Peng Yu,Xue Yang,Lei Zhao,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出文档介导的强化学习（DMRL）框架，通过将技能文档优化建模为结构化编辑动作序列，利用双层代理机制（上层编辑、下层评估）结合双重相对策略优化（DRPO）和长期奖励预测器（LRP），解决了广告推荐中技能优化缺乏原则性奖励归因的问题，并在大规模短视频广告平台上显著提升了关键指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的利用大语言模型（LLM）辅助广告推荐参数调优的方法主要依赖提示驱动（prompt-driven），缺乏将奖励归因于特定文档编辑的原则性机制，难以有效平衡商业回报与用户体验。

Method: 提出DMRL框架，包含：1. 双层代理机制：上层代理执行受控文档编辑，冻结的下层任务代理通过A/B测试评估效果；2. 双重相对策略优化（DRPO）：用于鲁棒且风险感知的优势估计；3. 长期奖励预测器（LRP）：利用解耦表示学习和交叉注意力迁移建模群体异质性，以预测长期结果。

Result: 在大规模短视频广告平台上的部署和实证评估显示，DMRL在关键广告指标上优于现有的最先进（SOTA）基线方法。

Conclusion: DMRL通过结构化编辑动作和创新的强化学习组件，成功实现了技能文档的自我进化，有效解决了广告推荐系统中的复杂参数调优和长期奖励归因难题。

Abstract: Advertising recommendation requires continuously tuning complex system parameters while balancing commercial returns and user experience. Recent work has introduced large language models (LLMs) with skill documents to assist this labor-intensive process, but skill optimization remains largely prompt-driven, lacking a principled mechanism to attribute rewards to specific document edits. To address this limitation, we propose Document-Mediated Reinforcement Learning (DMRL), a skill self-evolution framework that models skill document optimization as a sequence of structured editing actions. In DMRL, an upper-level agent performs controlled document edits, while a frozen lower-level task agent evaluates their effects through A/B testing. To address credit assignment and long-term outcomes, we introduce two key components: (1) Dual-Relative Policy Optimization (DRPO), a post-training policy optimization method for robust and risk-aware advantage estimation; and (2) Long-term Reward Predictor (LRP), which estimates long-term outcomes by modeling population heterogeneity with disentangled representation learning and cross-attention transfer. DMRL was deployed on a large-scale short-video ads platform and extensive empirical evaluation shows that DMRL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across key advertising metrics

</details>


### [313] [Learning the Constitutive Behavior of Materials via Neural Operators and Causal Attention: Case Studies in Plasticity and Damage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02194)
*Rishabh Arora,Lisa Scheunemann,Tim Brepols,Shahed Rezaei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于材料算子概念的数据驱动本构建模框架，通过因果掩码注意力机制和谱卷积，实现了从完整应变历史到应力响应的函数到函数映射，能够准确预测不可逆变形机制，同时具有分辨率不变性和并行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的本构建模依赖于基于领域知识假设的内部状态变量及其演化方程，但在实际应用中，相关内部变量通常无法通过实验测量，本构响应必须完全从测量的应变 - 应力数据中推断，且无需任何关于材料内部状态的先验知识。

Method: 提出基于材料算子的数据驱动本构建模框架，将变形材料视为从其完整应变历史到相应应力响应的函数映射；采用因果掩码注意力机制强制时间路径依赖性，限制模型仅关注过去材料状态同时保持计算并行性；使用谱卷积提供频域中的离散化不变表示；采用正弦激活函数解决非弹性区域中固有的强非线性过渡。

Result: 在多维、率无关材料模型上进行了评估，重点关注非线性塑性和延性损伤累积；结果表明该框架能够准确且稳健地预测不可逆变形机制，同时实现分辨率不变性和优异的并行效率。

Conclusion: 该数据驱动本构建模框架通过材料算子概念，成功实现了从应变历史到应力响应的直接映射，克服了传统方法对内部状态变量的依赖，在复杂非弹性材料行为预测中表现出准确性、鲁棒性和计算效率优势。

Abstract: Classical constitutive modeling of path-dependent inelastic materials relies on internal state variables whose evolution equations must be postulated based on domain knowledge and calibrated against experimental data. However, in many practical settings, the relevant internal variables are typically not measurable in experiments, and the constitutive response must be inferred entirely from measured strain-stress data without any prior knowledge of the material's internal state. We propose a data-driven constitutive modeling framework based on the concept of a material operator, which treats a deforming material as a functional mapping from its entire strain history to the corresponding stress response. In contrast to traditional autoregressive or recurrent formulations, the model is trained directly on full loading paths as function-to-function mappings, predicting complete stress trajectories in a single parallel forward pass. Temporal path dependence is enforced through a causally masked attention mechanism embedded within the operator, which restricts the model's attention to past material states while preserving computational parallelizability. Spectral convolutions provide discretization-invariant representations in the frequency domain, while causal attention captures highly adaptive, non-local history dependence. Furthermore, sinusoidal activation functions are used to resolve the strong nonlinear transitions inherent in inelastic regimes. The framework is evaluated across multidimensional, rate-independent material models exhibiting complex phenomena, with an emphasis on nonlinear plasticity and ductile damage accumulation. The results demonstrate accurate and robust predictions of irreversible deformation mechanisms while simultaneously achieving resolution invariance and excellent parallel efficiency.

</details>


### [314] [SMart: A Multi-source Multi-phase Time Series Representation Transfer Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02203)
*Fang He,Wang-chien Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的时间序列表示学习框架SMart，通过多阶段递归图恢复任务和源数据集选择器机制，解决了现有方法在自监督任务和单一数据源方面的不足，在多个任务上优于现有模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前时间序列表示学习（TSRL）存在两个主要问题：1）基于transformer的框架中自监督时间序列恢复任务技术简单；2）表示迁移仅使用单一源数据集。这两个方面都有改进空间。

Method: 提出SMart框架，包含两个创新机制：1）多阶段递归图恢复任务（三种替代模式），引导编码器将时间序列动态嵌入表示中；2）源数据集选择器，选择多个合适的源数据集补充目标数据集进行预训练。

Result: 在单变量和多变量时间序列数据集上，SMart在表示学习、分类和回归任务上均优于多个最先进模型：时间序列回归平均绝对误差降低最多19.5%，时间序列分类平均准确率提升最多1.34%。

Conclusion: SMart框架通过多阶段恢复任务和多源数据选择机制有效提升了时间序列表示学习性能，证明了多源多阶段迁移策略的有效性。

Abstract: Time series representation learning (TSRL) has attracted growing research interests in recent years. Two recent explorations in TSRL are: i) exploiting a transformer-based framework to learn time series; ii) instead of using only the targeted dataset, borrowing time series from other datasets to to facilitate representation transfer. While these two explorations are shown effective, the self-supervised time series recovery task in (i) and the single-source dataset used in (ii) are technically simple and thus can be enhanced with new ideas. In this work, we propose a new TSRL framework, namely multi-source multi-phase time series representation transfer (SMart), which has two novel mechanisms to address the aforementioned deficiencies: 1) a multi-phase recurrence plots recovery task, in three alternative modes, for guiding the encoder to embed time series dynamics into the time series representation; and 2) a source dataset selector to select multiple suitable source datasets to supplement the original target dataset for pre-training the TSRL encoder. Experimental results show that SMart outperforms several state-of-the-art models for time series representation learning, classification and regression on both uni-variate and multi-variate time series datasets, reducing mean absolute error up to 19.5% for time series regression, and increasing average accuracy up to 1.34\% for time series classification.

</details>


### [315] [Recursive Value Learning for Long-Horizon Offline Goal-Conditioned RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02237)
*Hyeonseong Jeon,Youngwoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DCRL 通过递归地将轨迹分解为平衡二叉树结构，从叶子到根训练价值函数，将最坏情况下的自举深度从线性降低到对数级，从而显著提升了长视野离线目标条件强化学习的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将离线目标条件强化学习（GCRL）扩展到长视野任务存在困难，主要原因有二：一是长范围价值学习依赖于可能仍不准确的短范围估计；二是基于最大值的价值备份会通过重复传播放大高估误差。

Method: 提出 DCRL（分治强化学习），递归地将每个轨迹段分解为平衡二叉树，并从叶子节点到根节点训练价值函数。父节点仅在子节点更新后进行更新，使用观测路径的精确分解而非在噪声替代方案中进行选择。同时，DCRL 在轨迹间联合传播价值以发现更短路径。

Result: 在多种目标到达任务中，DCRL 显著优于先前的扁平离线 GCRL 方法。在五个最具挑战性的长视野 OGBench 任务上，将最佳先前平均分数从 55 提升至 64，超越了所有扁平化和分层基线。

Conclusion: 通过平衡二叉树结构，DCRL 将最坏情况下的自举深度从线性降低到对数级，这种更短的依赖结构在经验上对应于更慢的误差累积，有效解决了长视野任务中的价值学习难题。

Abstract: Scaling offline goal-conditioned reinforcement learning (GCRL) to long-horizon tasks is difficult because (1) long-range value learning depends on shorter-range estimates that may still be inaccurate, and (2) max-based value backups can amplify overestimation through repeated propagation. We propose DCRL (Divide-and-Conquer RL), which recursively decomposes each trajectory segment into a balanced binary tree and trains the values from leaves to root. Each parent is therefore updated only after its children, using an exact factorization of the observed route rather than selecting among noisy alternatives. Since this objective learns values along demonstrated routes that are not necessarily optimal, DCRL jointly propagates values across trajectories to discover shorter routes. Thanks to the balanced binary tree, DCRL reduces worst-case bootstrap depth from linear to logarithmic, and this shorter dependency structure empirically corresponds to much slower error accumulation. Across diverse goal-reaching tasks, DCRL substantially outperforms prior flat offline GCRL methods, and on the five most challenging long-horizon OGBench tasks, it improves the best prior average score from 55 to 64, surpassing all flat and hierarchical baselines.

</details>


### [316] [Similarity-Aware Personalized Federated Learning in Heterogeneous Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02241)
*Arun Kumar A,Sunil Gupta,Dang Ngyuen,Bao Duong,Dat Phan Trong*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SAPE-FL，一种新型个性化联邦学习框架，通过将客户端模型同时锚定到全局模型和相似度加权的同伴平均模型，解决了数据分布不匹配导致的性能下降问题，在高度异构和数据稀缺场景下优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决联邦学习中因客户端数据分布不匹配（统计异构性）导致的全局泛化能力差及本地性能下降问题，防止部分客户端本地模型表现优于全局模型从而削弱协作价值的情况。

Method: 提出SAPE-FL框架，采用双重锚定机制：将客户端模型锚定至全局模型及基于相似度加权的同伴平均模型；引入基于模型相似度和输出相似度的动态、客户端特异性正则化，自适应平衡全局知识迁移与同伴协作，并过滤不相似客户端。

Result: 理论分析证明了算法的收敛性保证；实证结果表明，在高统计异构性和低客户端数据量条件下，SAPE-FL的性能优于当前最先进（SOTA）的方法。

Conclusion: SAPE-FL通过双重锚定机制有效缓解了负迁移问题，增强了联邦学习在异构环境下的鲁棒性，成功实现了全局知识与个性化本地需求的自适应平衡。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) allows decentralized clients to train models collaboratively while preserving data privacy. However, distribution mismatch across clients often leads to poor global generalization and degraded local client-level performance. In such scenarios, some of the clients with their local models trained solely on local data may perform better than the globally learnt model, thus nullifying the benefits of collaborative federated learning. To address this, we propose SAPE-FL (Similarity-Aware Personalized Federated Learning), a novel personalization framework that anchors each client's model to both the global model and a similarity-weighted peer averaged model. By incorporating dynamic, client-specific regularization based on both model similarity and output similarity, SAPE-FL adaptively balances global knowledge transfer and peer collaboration while filtering out dissimilar clients. This dual anchoring mitigates negative transfer and enhances robustness in heterogeneous settings. We theoretically analyze our algorithm establishing its convergence guarantees and empirically show that SAPE-FL outperforms state-of-the-art methods under high statistical heterogeneity and low client data regimes.

</details>


### [317] [CAPTURE: Disentangling Preference Drift from Memory Poisoning in Personalized LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02265)
*S M Asif Hossain,Ruksat Khan Shayoni,Md Kishor Morol*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出CAPTURE框架，通过神经微分方程信念追踪器和多时间尺度记忆账本解决个性化语言代理中的记忆中毒问题，在保持83.5%真实偏好更新接受率的同时将固定策略中毒成功率限制在11.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 个性化语言代理使用持久记忆适应用户，但该机制易受攻击。当新信息与存储偏好冲突时，代理需区分真实偏好漂移与临时上下文变化、歧义或对抗性记忆中毒，现有基于新近性和来源的规则不足。

Method: 将问题建模为潜在用户状态的连续时间部分可观测决策过程，采用神经微分方程信念追踪器、多时间尺度记忆账本、不确定性触发澄清和引用记忆反事实审计。

Result: 在96用户480集测试中，CAPTURE胜率71.5%，优于基线69.3%和启发式基线66.1%；限制固定策略中毒成功率11.5%，接受83.5%真实偏好更新；自适应攻击下成功率升至24.7%。

Conclusion: 显式建模偏好真实性可同时提升记忆增强LLM代理的个性化和鲁棒性，但存在适应性与安全性的权衡。

Abstract: Personalized language agents use persistent memory to adapt to users over time, but the same mechanism creates an attack surface. When new information conflicts with stored preferences, an agent must distinguish genuine preference drift from temporary context shifts, ambiguity, or adversarial memory poisoning. We formulate this problem as a continuous-time partially observable decision process over a latent user state and show why rules based only on recency and provenance are insufficient. CAPTURE addresses this ambiguity with a neural differential-equation belief tracker, a multi-timescale memory ledger, uncertainty-triggered clarification, and counterfactual auditing of cited memories. On 480 held-out episodes from 96 users, CAPTURE achieves a 71.5% win rate, compared with 69.3% for an identically supervised baseline and 66.1% for the strongest heuristic baseline. It limits fixed-policy poisoning success to 11.5% while accepting 83.5% of genuine preference updates. Under an adaptive attacker with access to the released weights, attack success rises to 24.7%, exposing a real adaptation-security tradeoff. We further evaluate the frozen system zero-shot on an independently constructed benchmark and replay longitudinal interaction histories from 40 users collected over two to three weeks. These results suggest that modeling preference authenticity explicitly can improve both personalization and robustness in memory-augmented LLM agents.

</details>


### [318] [Entangled Representations Amplify Collateral Damage in Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02285)
*Evžen Wybitul,Tim G. J. Rudner,Christian Schroeder de Witt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究通过控制实验验证了表征纠缠使机器遗忘更难的直觉，发现更解纠缠的模型在遗忘效果相同时，保留成本显著降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性研究中普遍认为表征纠缠（不同知识域间结构共享）会使机器遗忘更困难，但这一观点从未在受控实验中直接验证。

Method: 利用选择性梯度掩蔽（SGTM）训练六个2.54亿参数的语言模型，在英语维基百科数据上设置不同等级的生物学与非生物学知识解纠缠程度，并对每个模型应用三种标准遗忘方法进行测试。

Result: 更解纠缠的模型 consistently 实现更好的保留 - 遗忘权衡：在固定遗忘水平下，最解纠缠模型在两种方法下保留成本降低约 4 倍，在第三种方法下降低 1.3 倍。

Conclusion: 表征纠缠确实是导致机器遗忘中附带损害的原因之一，该实验设计可用于验证可解释性研究中的其他结构性主张。

Abstract: A long-held intuition in interpretability research is that representational entanglement, the sharing of structure between knowledge domains in a neural network, makes unlearning harder. While the intuition is widespread, it has never been directly tested in a controlled experiment. We present a way to do so: by repurposing Selective Gradient Masking (SGTM), we train a suite of six 254M-parameter language models on English Wikipedia with graded levels of disentanglement between biology and non-biology knowledge. Applying three standard unlearning methods to every model in the suite, we find that more disentangled models consistently achieve better retain-forget trade-offs: at a fixed level of forgetting, the most disentangled models incur roughly $4\times$ lower retain cost under two of the three methods, and $1.3\times$ lower under the third. Because our intervention changes only the model, not the data or the unlearning algorithm, this is direct evidence that representational entanglement is one of the causes of collateral damage in unlearning, as interpretability researchers have long suspected. A similar design could be used to test other structural claims from interpretability.

</details>


### [319] [SEAL: Reinforcing Global Safety in Mixture-of-Experts through Shared Expert ALignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02293)
*Qingyu Meng,Yiwei Zha,Jiahuan Pei,Koen Hindriks,Herbert Bos,Min Chen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对混合专家模型（MoE）因稀疏路由机制导致的安全漏洞，提出了一种基于共享专家（Shared Experts）的防御方法SEAL。该方法通过训练共享专家中的安全关键神经元，作为独立于路由的“锚点”来增强全局安全性，在显著降低攻击成功率的同时，仅带来极小的性能损耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE架构虽然高效，但其稀疏路由机制引入了结构性安全漏洞：攻击者可通过越狱提示、恶意微调或剪枝安全神经元来操纵路由选择，从而绕过现有的路由器加固防御。由于路由过程的非确定性，单纯硬化路由器无法完全阻止攻击路径被操纵或绕过。

Method: 提出SEAL及其变体SEAL++。核心洞察是利用始终激活的“共享专家”中包含的安全关键神经元，作为克服稀疏路由不确定性的路由器无关锚点。SEAL是一种训练时的参数高效防御方法，生成附加在共享专家上的即插即用适配器；SEAL++进一步引入正交约束，以在训练期间保留现有的安全子空间。

Result: 在结合三种对抗输入（有害提示、越狱、恶意微调）及神经元剪枝的六种攻击场景评估中，SEAL将攻击成功率（ASR）最高降低了60%，而在五个基准测试上的平均能力成本损失不超过1.4%。

Conclusion: 利用共享专家作为安全锚点能有效解决MoE架构中稀疏路由带来的不确定性问题。SEAL和SEAL++提供了一种高效、即插即用的防御方案，不仅能显著抵御多种攻击，还能无缝集成到现有的路由器级防御中，提升了MoE模型的整体鲁棒性。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) is a scaling architecture for large language models that activates only a small subset of expert modules per token, enabling massive parameter growth with nearly constant computation. Recent Hybrid MoE architecture adds \textit{shared experts} to capture consistently useful representations, further improving stability and generalization. MoE now powers many flagship open-source and commercial models, yet remains vulnerable to adversarial attacks. Specifically, sparse routing introduces a structural vulnerability: MoE safety hinges on which experts are activated, and adversaries can subvert this selection through jailbreak prompts, malicious fine-tuning, and weight-level pruning of safety-critical neurons. Existing defenses primarily focus on hardening the router, but an adversary may still manipulate or bypass the routing trajectory due to the routing process's nondeterministic nature, thereby collapsing the defense. To cope with this problem, we first identify theoretically and empirically that shared expert, an always-activated component containing a small proportion of safety-critical neurons, can overcome the uncertainty of sparsely activated routing path and serve as a router-independent anchor to enhance global safety alignment. Based on this insight, we propose SEAL, a training-time parameter-efficient defense that produces a plug-and-play adapter attached to shared expert, and SEAL++, a variant that adds an orthogonal constraint preserving pre-existing safety subspaces during training. We evaluate SEAL and SEAL++ across six attack scenarios that combine three adversarial inputs (harmful prompting, jailbreak, malicious fine-tuning) with and without neuron pruning. SEAL reduces attack success rate (ASR) by up to 60\%, at a capability cost of at most 1.4\% on a five-benchmark average. Additionally, SEAL can seamlessly integrate with router-level ......

</details>


### [320] [What Is Worth Representing? Representational Empowerment for Continual Model Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02322)
*Fei Dai,Hanqi Zhou,Alison Gopnik,Charley Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出表征赋能（RepEmp）框架，用于解决持续模型构建中“应表示什么”的问题，通过分层策展人 - 行动者架构，在因果学习和规划任务中验证其优于信息增益等基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建模世界的首要问题不仅是估计参数或因果结构，而是决定应表示什么内容。现有方法缺乏对可重用表征元素的选择机制，难以在资源受限下实现跨环境的持续模型构建。

Method: 提出表征赋能（RepEmp）评分机制，衡量候选元素对代理未来建模和规划能力的扩展程度；实现分层策展人 - 行动者架构，在封闭词汇因果学习、模拟实验和开放词汇规划域中进行测试。

Result: 人类实验显示 RepEmp 能更好预测抽象粒度选择；模拟表明 RepEmp 比探索更利于结构恢复和跨任务迁移；LLM 增强策展人构建更紧凑且泛化更好的符号库，消融 RepEmp 后优势消失。

Conclusion: RepEmp 是持续模型构建的关键原则，能有效指导在资源受限下决定构建、保留和重用哪些表征元素，提升建模效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: The first problem of modeling the world is not just estimating the right parameters or causal structure, but deciding what should be represented at all. We frame this problem as continual model construction: an agent maintains an environment-specific model M of an inaccessible world W and curates a persistent library L of reusable representational elements across environments. We propose Representational Empowerment (RepEmp) to score candidate elements by how much they expand the agent's future capacity to model and plan, complementing the classic definition of empowerment, but redefined as control over internal representations instead of external states. We realize the framework as a hierarchical Curator-Actor architecture and test it across three experiments. In a closed-vocabulary causal-learning task, human participants construct causal models at varying abstraction granularities to maximize goal reachability rather than fidelity to the world, a signature better predicted by RepEmp than by information-gain alternatives. Matched simulations reveal that RepEmp-guided construction contributes more than exploration to sufficient structure recovery and cross-task transfer. Finally, in an open-vocabulary planning domain, an LLM-augmented Curator builds more compact symbolic libraries, which also generalize better than baselines. Ablating RepEmp eliminates these benefits. Together, these results identify RepEmp as a key principle for continual model construction: deciding what to build, retain, and reuse under bounded resources.

</details>


### [321] [AGI Maze Prediction Datasets: A Compact Benchmark for Learning World Dynamics with Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02339)
*Alexey Potapov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了AGI迷宫预测数据集与基准，用于测试Transformer等模型的世界建模能力。研究发现，具有结构化空间记忆机制的模型（伪视频空间记忆Transformer）在泛化性和长程预测任务上显著优于仅增加潜在容量的模型或纯字节级Transformer，证明了结构化工作记忆对于语言接地和状态推理的重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界建模要求预测模型能够维护并更新内部状态以推理行动后果。现有研究缺乏轻量级、可控的测试环境来区分模型是真正学习了可迁移的动作条件动态，还是仅仅记住了特定布局的转换。因此，需要构建一个基准来评估模型在结构化状态推理和语言接地方面的能力。

Method: 构建了源自程序化生成的有状态网格世界的“AGI迷宫预测数据集与基准”，包含单步转换预测、固定视界状态预测和序列文本观察预测任务。采用源迷宫不相交的训练/验证划分和贪婪精确匹配评估。对比了三种架构：从头训练的字节级Transformer基线、通用辅助潜在记忆Transformer，以及伪视频空间记忆Transformer（后者初始化二维潜在工作空间并仅根据动作历史更新，不接收中间地图或状态标签）。

Result: 通用辅助潜在记忆Transformer虽能完美拟合部分训练集，但未能一致提升持出集（held-out）性能。相比之下，伪视频空间记忆Transformer在相同的数居、目标和评估协议下，在选定的固定视界任务上达到了完美的验证准确率（基线模型未能达到），并显著提升了序列文本轨迹预测的性能。

Conclusion: 结构化且与任务对齐的工作记忆比单纯的额外潜在容量更有用。语言接地是通过持久化的数据结构及其上的计算来实现的；该基准为测试将文本接口与学习到的结构化状态相结合的架构提供了一个紧凑且有效的环境。

Abstract: World modeling requires a predictive model to maintain and update an internal state adequate for reasoning about the consequences of actions. We introduce the AGI Maze Prediction Datasets and Benchmark, a lightweight controlled testbed for studying this capability in Transformers and other predictive models. Derived from procedurally generated, stateful grid worlds, the benchmark comprises per-step transition prediction, fixed-horizon state prediction, and sequential textual-observation prediction. Source-maze-disjoint training and validation splits, together with greedy exact-match evaluation, distinguish learning transferable action-conditioned dynamics from memorizing transitions in familiar layouts. We establish from-scratch byte-level Transformer baselines and compare them with two working-memory-augmented architectures. A generic auxiliary latent-memory Transformer can fit some training sets perfectly but does not consistently improve held-out performance. In contrast, a pseudo-video spatial-memory Transformer initializes a two-dimensional latent workspace from the input map and updates it from action history without receiving intermediate maps, positions, or state labels. Under the same data, objectives, and evaluation protocol, this model reaches perfect validation accuracy on selected fixed-horizon tasks where the byte and unstructured-memory baselines do not, and substantially improves sequential text-trace prediction. These results suggest that structured, task-aligned working memory can be more useful than additional latent capacity alone. More broadly, we argue that language grounding is mediated by persistent data structures and computations over them; the benchmark offers a compact setting for testing architectures that couple textual interfaces to learned structured state.

</details>


### [322] [Percolation Dynamics in Optimization : Variance Cascades and Discrete Scale Invariance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02373)
*Sai Niranjan Ramachandran,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过将随机梯度流建模为渗流过程，揭示了SGD及Adam/AdamW优化器在训练深度神经网络时，因架构对称性导致子网络以离散块形式合并的机制，并指出这种结构转变表现为宏观序参数的方差尖峰，类似于物理相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管已知随机梯度下降（SGD）会将深度神经网络导向对应于更简单子网络的不变集，但这一引导过程随时间展开的具体动态机制尚不清楚，缺乏深入理解。

Method: 将随机梯度流（SGF）建模为渗流过程（percolation process），分析架构对称性如何迫使子网络以离散的同步块形式合并；同时在显式的重尾噪声模型下，验证该机制在Adam和AdamW优化器中的扩展性。

Result: 发现结构转变会在宏观序参数中注册为方差尖峰，这种现象回声了物理系统中的相变；证实了这种捕获机制及其相关的缩放级联不仅适用于SGD，也延伸至Adam和AdamW优化器。

Conclusion: 深度神经网络的优化动力学可以通过渗流理论和相变视角来理解，架构对称性导致的离散块合并是优化过程中的关键结构特征，且该机制在多种主流优化器中具有普适性。

Abstract: We study the dynamics of Stochastic Gradient Descent (SGD), which is known to steer deep neural networks toward invariant sets that correspond to simpler subnetworks. How this steering unfolds over time remains poorly understood. We answer this by modeling the stochastic gradient flow (SGF) as a percolation process, in which architectural symmetries force subnetworks to merge in discrete simultaneous blocks rather than one at a time. These structural transitions register as variance spikes in a macroscopic order parameter, echoing physical phase transitions. We further show this trapping mechanism and its associated scaling cascade extend to Adam and AdamW under an explicit heavy-tailed noise model.

</details>


### [323] [Evidence for Shared Routing Geometry and Dynamics in Sparse Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02404)
*Kirill Labzin,Stepan Kulibaba,Artem Dzhalilov,Artem Gorokhov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现稀疏混合专家（MoE）模型中各层的路由状态存在被坐标系掩盖的共享几何结构，通过广义正交普鲁克分析对齐后，单一线性转移即可有效预测跨层路由动态，且该动态具有路由特异性而非单纯的隐藏表示平滑演化，利用预测状态替代原生状态可显著降低负对数似然损失。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的研究表明MoE模型中跨深度的路由决策具有可预测性，暗示路由并非完全独立，但其背后的结构机制尚不明确。本文旨在揭示这种可预测性背后的几何结构，并探究其是否反映了跨层可复用的路由演化过程。

Method: 1. 隔离每个路由器的控制子空间；2. 利用广义正交普鲁克分析（generalized orthogonal Procrustes analysis）将这些子空间对齐到共享的规范表示中；3. 拟合单一线性转移模型以评估预测能力；4. 通过匹配秩比较（matched-rank comparison）区分通用跨层可预测性与路由特异性信息；5. 测试预测的规范状态在替代原生路由状态时的有效性。

Result: 1. 对齐后，单一线性转移的$R^2$达到0.39–0.71，保留了79–90%的独立拟合层特定动态的预测能力；2. 残差表示虽更易预测，但路由器控制状态能更忠实地保留模型的专家选择，证明了路由特异性；3. 在OLMoE上，学习到的状态演化相比简单持久性使$\Delta\mathrm{NLL}$降低了15.7%，在Phi模型上跨越10个路由器范围降低了6.2%。

Conclusion: MoE模型中的路由状态演化遵循跨深度可复用的共享动态过程，该过程具有特定的几何结构且包含区别于通用隐藏表示演化的路由特异性信息；利用对齐后的规范状态进行预测能有效维持局部路由行为并提升模型性能。

Abstract: Sparse mixture-of-experts (MoE) models use an independently parameterized router at each sparse layer to select experts for every token. Prior work has shown that routing decisions across depth can often be predicted from earlier routing signals, suggesting that routing is not fully independent across layers. However, the structure behind this predictability remains unclear. In this work, we provide evidence that routing-relevant states across layers share a common geometric structure that is obscured by layer-specific coordinate systems. We isolate the control subspace of each router and align these spaces into a shared canonical representation using generalized orthogonal Procrustes analysis. After alignment, a single linear transition reaches $R^2=0.39$--$0.71$ and retains 79--90\% of the predictive power of separately fitted layer-specific dynamics, indicating that much of routing-state evolution follows a reusable process across depth. We then ask whether this shared dynamics is specific to routing or simply reflects the smooth evolution of hidden representations. A matched-rank comparison shows that residual representations are often easier to predict across layers, while router-control states preserve the model's expert choices much more faithfully. This separates generic cross-layer predictability from routing-specific information. Finally, we test whether the predicted canonical states remain meaningful when used in place of native routing states. The transported states preserve local routing behavior, while learned state evolution reduces $Δ\mathrm{NLL}$ relative to simple persistence by 15.7\% on OLMoE and 6.2\% over a 10-router horizon on Phi.

</details>


### [324] [Coverage, Not Targeting: A Structural Regime in Multi-Turn Agent Credit Assignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02417)
*Chenyu Zhou,Qiliang Jiang,Shuning Wu,Xu Zhou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出在多轮代理强化学习中，针对终端验证器的低信息密度场景，传统的“信用分配靶向”策略（即仅奖励关键步骤）效果不如“均匀密集奖励”，因为后者能更好地覆盖长因果链所需的先决条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多将多轮代理强化学习中的信用分配视为“靶向问题”，试图将终端奖励 localize 到关键步骤。然而，作者发现当验证器信息密度（$V_d$）较低时（即验证器仅暴露因果链中极少部分的正确性），这种靶向策略是错误的优化方向，导致模型难以学习到完整的长链条工具调用序列。

Method: 1. 提出结构量“验证器信息密度”$V_d = k/C$，用于预测靶向策略的有效性。
2. 在 tau^2-bench 和 BFCL V3 基准上进行受控的共享 rollout 对比实验，分离奖励密度与信用几何结构。
3. 对比三种奖励策略：均匀密集奖励、针对进步步骤的集中奖励、针对随机步骤的集中奖励。
4. 在 ToolACE-2-8B 模型家族上进行多种子预注册实验及匹配预算的广度扫描，验证剂量 - 反应关系。

Result: 1. 在低$V_d$ regime（tau^2-bench 中$V_d \approx 0.15$）下，均匀密集奖励显著优于稀疏的二元终端奖励（5/5 种子中后者有害）。
2. 将奖励集中在“进步步骤”或“随机步骤”同样有害，证明“靶向”是二阶因素，核心机制在于“覆盖率”。
3. 终端状态验证将可观测信号坍缩至最后一步（98% rollout 中 k=1），而成功需要 5-8 步的先决条件链。
4. 理论相边界显示交叉点在$V_d^* \approx 0.8$，实测值远低于此。
5. 在 ToolACE-2-8B 上，靶向策略导致性能下降（$\Delta = -0.048$），且只有在实现全链条覆盖时，均匀奖励的劣势才会消失。

Conclusion: 在终端验证器信息密度较低的场景下，均匀重新分配奖励是“零信息覆盖”的默认最优解，优于任何试图进行 per-turn 靶向的方案。靶向策略仅在验证器能覆盖大部分因果链（高$V_d$）时才有效。论文提出了“匹配浓度洗牌控制”作为评估任何靶向声称的必要基准。

Abstract: Multi-turn agentic RL increasingly treats credit assignment as a targeting problem: given a terminal verifiable reward, per-turn methods localize credit onto the turns that mattered. We identify the structural quantity that predicts when this is the right move, the verifier information density V_d = k/C (the fraction of an agent's C-step causal chain whose per-turn correctness the verifier exposes), and show that terminal-state verifiers sit deep in a low-V_d regime where targeting is the wrong axis. In controlled shared-rollout comparisons on tau^2-bench that separate reward density from credit geometry, a continuous dense reward spread uniformly beats the sparse binary outcome reward (net-harmful on 4/5 seeds), while concentrating the same advantage on progress turns or on random turns is equally harmful: targeting is second-order. The mechanism is coverage: terminal-state verification collapses the observable signal to a single final-write turn (k=1 in 98% of rollouts) while success requires a 5-8 step chain of prerequisite tool calls. A synthetic phase boundary places the crossover at V_d* ~ 0.8, whereas measured V_d is ~0.15 on tau^2-bench and ~0.4 on BFCL V3; uniform also wins on BFCL, where a matched-concentration shuffled control is negative on 8/8 seeds. The effect reproduces across model families on ToolACE-2-8B (Delta = -0.048 over 32 pre-registered seeds; an independent 20-seed replication is itself significant), and a pre-registered matched-budget breadth sweep traces a monotone dose-response whose deficit vanishes only at full chain coverage, with a reward-to-go arm reaching full-coverage parity. Uniform redistribution is the zero-information coverage default that per-turn schemes must beat; we contribute the matched-concentration shuffled control that any targeting claim should clear.

</details>


### [325] [IFW-BLS: Dual-Robust Broad Learning System with Intuitionistic Fuzzy Wave Loss](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02422)
*Mushir Akhtar,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出IFW-BLS模型，通过引入直觉模糊波损失函数和样本可信度控制机制，解决传统Broad Learning System对噪声、异常值及标签错误敏感的问题，实验表明其在UCI数据集上性能优于基线模型且更稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统Broad Learning System（BLS）采用标准最小二乘训练，存在两个脆弱性：1）噪声、异常值或错误标签导致的大残差会主导目标函数；2）所有样本被视为同等可靠，忽略了部分样本位于模糊或局部冲突区域的情况。

Method: 提出IFW-BLS模型，包含两个鲁棒性机制：1）残差级保护，用有界、平滑、非对称的波损失函数替代平方损失；2）样本级可信度控制，通过结合全局类中心一致性与局部邻域冲突的直觉模糊评分。使用Nesterov加速梯度优化器求解，避免传统BLS的显式矩阵求逆。

Result: 在UCI基准数据集上的实验验证了IFW-BLS优于基线模型；额外的数据污染实验表明，在噪声和异常值干扰下，IFW-BLS比传统BLS表现更稳定。

Conclusion: IFW-BLS通过单一优化模型同时解决残差主导和样本可靠性问题，有效提升了BLS在噪声环境下的鲁棒性和稳定性。

Abstract: Broad Learning System is an efficient randomized learning model that expands network width through feature and enhancement nodes and estimates the output weights without deep backpropagation. Its standard least-squares training, however, is vulnerable in two different ways: (i) large residuals caused by noise, outliers, or corrupted labels can dominate the objective, and (ii) all samples are treated as equally reliable even when some lie in ambiguous or locally conflicting regions. This paper proposes IFW-BLS, an Intuitionistic Fuzzy Wave Broad Learning System that addresses these two sources of fragility within one optimization model. The first robustness mechanism is residual-level protection, obtained by replacing the squared loss with the bounded, smooth, and asymmetric wave loss. Boundedness prevents extreme residuals from receiving unbounded influence, while asymmetry allows positive and negative deviations to be penalized differently when the dominant error direction varies. The second mechanism is sample-level credibility control, obtained through intuitionistic fuzzy scores that combine global class-center consistency with local neighborhood conflict. The resulting model evaluates the wave loss on credibility-weighted residuals, so unreliable samples are down-weighted before the bounded loss further limits the effect of extreme errors. A Nesterov accelerated gradient based optimizer is used to solve the proposed objective, avoiding the explicit matrix inversion used in conventional BLS. Experiments on UCI benchmark datasets validate the superiority of the proposed IFW-BLS model over the baseline models; additional corruption experiments also show more stable performance than BLS under noise and outlier contamination.

</details>


### [326] [Towards One-for-All Robustness Across a Continuum of Threat Levels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02440)
*Zhichao Hou,Xiaorui Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出威胁条件网络（TCN），通过表示分解框架，在单一模型中实现了对连续威胁水平的自适应鲁棒性，仅需4.6%的参数开销即可匹敌多个专用模型的集成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对抗鲁棒模型常过拟合于特定的攻击预算，导致在面对多样且动态的对抗环境时需维护多个专用模型，随着威胁空间扩大，该策略变得不可行。核心挑战在于能否在单一模型中实现跨越连续威胁水平的强鲁棒性。

Method: 提出威胁条件网络（TCN），基于表示分解框架，将表示学习分解为威胁不变的共享骨干网络和轻量级威胁条件适配器。利用基于傅里叶的嵌入和通道级仿射调制，使模型根据扰动水平进行条件化，并在扰动预算分布上进行训练，从而在推理时灵活适应无限连续的威胁水平。

Result: 在CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100和Tiny-ImageNet上的实验表明，TCN以单一参数集达到或超越了预算专用模型全集成的性能，能泛化至未见过的扰动预算，并在威胁条件不匹配时保持鲁棒迁移能力，参数开销仅为4.6%。

Conclusion: TCN为在动态多样的威胁环境中实现自适应且可泛化的鲁棒性提供了一条富有前景的技术路径。

Abstract: Adversarially robust models often overfit to a specific attack budget, necessitating multiple specialized models for diverse and dynamic adversarial environments, a strategy that becomes fundamentally intractable as the threat space grows. This raises an open challenge: can we achieve strong robustness across a continuum of threat levels within a single model? We propose the Threat Conditional Network (TCN), grounded in a representation factorization framework that decomposes representation learning into a threat-invariant shared backbone and a lightweight threat-conditional adaptor. TCN conditions a single model on the perturbation level via Fourier-based embeddings and channel-wise affine modulation, and is trained against a distribution over perturbation budgets, enabling flexible and seamless adaptation across an infinite continuum of threat levels during inference. Extensive experiments on CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, and Tiny-ImageNet show that TCN matches or surpasses a full ensemble of budget-specialized models with a single set of parameters, generalizes to unseen perturbation budgets, and transfers robustly under mismatched threat conditions, with only 4.6\% parameter overhead. These contributions chart a promising path toward adaptive and generalizable robustness in dynamic and diverse threat environments.

</details>


### [327] [CACTUS: Mask-Guided Semantic Clean-Label Backdoors in Decentralized Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02450)
*Chao Feng,Burkhard Stiller*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CACTUS 是一种针对去中心化联邦学习（DFL）的后门攻击方法，通过将标签一致的语义对转换为目标导向的表示偏移，并在聚合前对干净样本进行反事实应用，实现跨模态的高效后门传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义触发器在联邦学习中比合成补丁更隐蔽，但样本依赖的放置可能削弱跨聚合轮次的后门植入；在去中心化联邦学习（DFL）中，拓扑依赖的节点聚合进一步加剧了该挑战。

Method: CACTUS 将标签一致的语义对转换为目标导向的表示偏移；采用掩码引导、模态特定的算子隔离触发器效应，跨样本耦合，并在节点聚合前对干净非目标嵌入进行反事实应用。

Result: 在语音、文本、表格和图像四种模态、九种聚合规则下实验，30% 恶意节点时，CACTUS 在 Speech Commands 上平均攻击成功率（ASR）达 51.2%，在四种模态中的三种上达到所有评估攻击中的最高平均 ASR；ASR 随网络拓扑变化，且随恶意节点比例增加而上升。

Conclusion: CACTUS 能够通过重复的 DFL 聚合有效传播后门，证明其在去中心化联邦学习环境下的攻击有效性。

Abstract: Semantic triggers in federated learning (FL) can be less conspicuous than synthetic patches, but sample-dependent placement may weaken backdoor implantation across aggregation rounds. This challenge is compounded in decentralized FL (DFL), where topology-dependent peer aggregation repeatedly mixes local models. CACTUS converts label-consistent semantic pairs into target-directed representation shifts. Mask-guided, modality-specific operators isolate trigger effects, couple them across samples, and apply the shifts counterfactually to clean non-target embeddings before peer aggregation. Experiments cover speech, text, tabular, and image tasks under nine aggregation rules. With 30\% malicious nodes, CACTUS reaches a nine-rule mean attack success rate (ASR) of 51.2\% on Speech Commands and the highest nine-rule mean ASR among evaluated attacks on three of four modalities. Sensitivity analyses show that ASR varies with network topology and increases with the malicious-node ratio. These results indicate that CACTUS can propagate backdoors through repeated DFL aggregation.

</details>


### [328] [Scalable Kronecker-Fisher Approximation: Efficient Hessian Analysis for Billion-Parameter Language Models Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02451)
*Viacheslav Yusupov,Daria Cherniuk,Evgeny Frolov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种可扩展的基于Kronecker的近似方法，无需存储完整的Fisher矩阵即可捕捉跨层交互，从而实现了对十亿参数网络的实用Hessian分析，揭示了值投影层的高敏感性，并验证了该方法在模型压缩与优化中的指导价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在十亿参数规模的大型神经网络中，由于计算和存储开销过大导致无法进行完整Hessian矩阵分析的问题，旨在寻找一种实用的方法来识别模型中的脆弱组件以指导模型压缩和优化。

Method: 提出一种基于Kronecker积的近似算法，通过避免存储完整的Fisher信息矩阵来捕捉层间交互；并在量化、稀疏化、层间损坏及损坏后微调等多种实验场景下验证该近似方法与性能退化及恢复的相关性。

Result: 研究发现值投影层（value projection layers）表现出最高的敏感性和最强的跨层相关性，而其他组件则呈现架构特异性行为；实验表明该近似方法与模型性能退化及恢复程度高度相关。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了一种实用且理论扎实的 outil，用于识别大型模型中的脆弱组件，为混合精度分配、层间稀疏性及自适应低秩分解等guided压缩和优化策略开辟了新途径。

Abstract: In this paper, we propose a scalable Kronecker-based approximation that captures cross-layer interactions without storing the entire Fisher matrix, enabling practical Hessian analysis for billion-parameter networks where full computation is infeasible. Our approach reveals consistent vulnerability patterns: value projection layers exhibit the highest sensitivity and strongest cross-layer correlations across multiple model families, while other components exhibit architecture-specific behaviors. Through extensive experiments on quantization, sparsification, inter-layer corruption, and post-corruption fine-tuning, we demonstrate that our approximation strongly correlates with both performance degradation and recovery. Our framework provides a practical, theoretically grounded tool for identifying fragile components in large models, opening new avenues for guided compression and optimization strategies, such as mixed-precision allocation, layer-wise sparsity, and adaptive low-rank decomposition across layers and even individual weight groups.

</details>


### [329] [DeepAffinity: Long-Term Aspect Preference Prediction in eCommerce using Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02468)
*Yotam Eshel,Guy Hadad,Guy Feigenblat,Yuri M. Brovman,Matt Gearhart,Bracha Shapira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 DeepAffinity 模型，利用小型语言模型（SLMs）和结构化提示预测电商用户对商品属性（如品牌、尺寸、颜色）的偏好，提升个性化推荐效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决电商场景中对用户商品属性偏好（Aspect Affinity）的预测问题，以增强客户理解并实现推荐、搜索和营销中的细粒度个性化。

Method: 将 Aspect Affinity 定义为时序预测任务，提出 DeepAffinity 模型，采用小型语言模型（SLMs）结合结构化提示和针对该任务微调的专用预测头。

Result: DeepAffinity 优于标准生成式微调方法；通用开源大语言模型未经任务特定微调时表现不佳；该模型在大型跨国电商平台上提升了推荐质量。

Conclusion: DeepAffinity 有效捕捉用户随时间演变的长期属性偏好，证明了任务特定微调对建模细微行为的重要性，并成功应用于实际电商推荐系统。

Abstract: We explore predicting eCommerce user preferences for product aspects such as brand, size, and color - a task we define as Aspect Affinity. Solving this task improves customer understanding and enables fine-grained personalization in recommendation, search, and marketing. We frame Aspect Affinity as a temporal prediction task: forecasting a users future aspect choices from their time-ordered interaction history, capturing long-term preferences that evolve beyond the current session. To this end, we propose DeepAffinity, which leverages Small Language Models (SLMs) with structured prompts and specialized prediction heads fine-tuned for this task. We show DeepAffinity outperforms standard generative fine-tuning methods, while general-purpose open-source LLMs perform poorly without task-specific tuning, highlighting their limits in modeling nuanced behavior. Finally, DeepAffinity enhances recommendation quality on a large-scale multinational eCommerce platform.

</details>


### [330] [RINSE: Robust Target-Time Normality Estimation for Zero-Shot Graph Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02497)
*Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Md Abrar Jahin,Amir Hussain*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了RINSE，一种无需梯度的零样本图异常检测框架，通过在目标图上迭代估计正常性、校准表示并融合可靠性证据，有效解决了域偏移问题，在八个未见目标图上取得了最优的平均AUPRC性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 零样本图异常检测旨在将源图训练的检测器部署到未见且无标签的目标图上，但域偏移（domain shift）会导致源域衍生的“正常性”概念在目标域中不可靠，亟需一种无需目标标签或微调的鲁棒适应方法。

Method: 提出RINSE（鲁棒迭代正常性自估计）框架：保持源训练检测器固定，在目标时间（target-time） sequentially 估计目标正常性、表示校准和证据可靠性；核心策略是识别低残差的目标节点子集构建修剪后的目标感知正常性模型，并通过可靠性门控的秩融合（reliability-gated rank fusion）与编码器集成来结合互补的异常证据。

Result: 在八个未见目标图上，RINSE在两种不同的预处理协议下均取得了所有评估方法中最高的平均AUPRC；块消融实验和敏感性分析验证了其组合设计的有效性。

Conclusion: 鲁棒的目标时间估计是一种实用的通用图异常检测方法，能够在无需目标标签、梯度计算或针对每个目标进行调优的情况下，有效应对域偏移挑战。

Abstract: Zero-shot graph anomaly detection seeks to deploy a detector trained on source graphs to unseen, unlabeled targets, yet domain shift can make source-derived notions of normality unreliable. We introduce RINSE (Robust Iterative Normality Self-Estimation), a gradient-free target-time framework that keeps the source-trained detector fixed while sequentially estimating target normality, representation calibration, and evidence reliability from the target graph. Its core idea is to identify a reliable subset of low-residual target nodes, use them to construct a trimmed target-aware normality model, and combine complementary anomaly evidence through reliability-gated rank fusion and encoder ensembling. Across eight unseen target graphs, RINSE achieves the highest average AUPRC among the evaluated methods under two separate preprocessing protocols, while block ablations and sensitivity analyses support the combined design. These results support robust target-time estimation as a practical approach to generalist graph anomaly detection without target labels, gradients, or per-target tuning.

</details>


### [331] [Rethinking the Teacher-Student Framework for Test-Time Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02507)
*Damian Sójka,Marc Masana,Bartłomiej Twardowski,Sebastian Cygert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文挑战了测试时适应（TTA）中教师 - 学生框架的常见策略，提出使用不更新权重的“顽固教师”（intransigent teacher），显著提升了长序列场景下的性能和鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的测试时适应（TTA）方法广泛采用教师 - 学生框架来缓解误差累积，但其长期稳定性常被忽视。研究表明，即使采用指数移动平均（EMA）设置教师权重，误差累积仍会在长序列中显现，因此需要重新审视该框架的稳定性 - 可塑性权衡。

Method: 作者提出使用“顽固教师”（intransigent teacher），即教师权重不随学生更新而变化的策略，替代传统的指数移动平均（EMA）教师权重更新方式，并在多个数据集和长序列场景下验证该方法的有效性。

Result: 实验表明，使用顽固教师的 TTA 方法在多个数据集的长序列场景下性能显著提升，对超参数变化的鲁棒性增强，且可无缝应用于不同架构和实验设置（包括语义分割）。

Conclusion: 简单的“顽固教师”策略能有效解决 TTA 中的误差累积问题，提升长期稳定性和鲁棒性，为测试时适应提供了更可靠的框架设计方向。

Abstract: Test-Time Adaptation (TTA) has recently emerged as a promising strategy that allows the adaptation of pre-trained models to changing data distributions at deployment time, without access to any labels. To mitigate error accumulation, researchers have widely adopted the teacher-student framework, though its long-term stability is often taken for granted. In this work, we challenge the common strategy of setting the teacher weights to an exponential moving average of the student by showing that error accumulation still occurs, although it is mostly apparent on longer sequences compared to those commonly utilized. We analyze the stability-plasticity trade-off within the teacher-student framework and propose to use an intransigent teacher that does not update its weights. Surprisingly, we show that this simple change allows TTA methods to significantly improve their performance on multiple datasets with longer scenarios and result in increased robustness to changes in hyperparameters. Finally, we show that those changes can be seamlessly and effectively applied to various architectures and experimental setups, including semantic segmentation. The code is available at https://github.com/dmn-sjk/intransigent_teacher.

</details>


### [332] [Spectral Initialization and Scheduled Graph Smoothness for Uncertain Knowledge Graph Completion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02519)
*Md Abrar Jahin,Taufikur Rahman Fuad,Jay Pujara,Craig A. Knoblock*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出QUEST方法，通过利用置信度加权图的谱结构先验（拉普拉斯特征向量初始化）和无偏小批量狄利克雷能量正则化，在无需增加可训练参数的情况下，显著提升了不确定知识图谱（UKG）的置信度预测与链接预测性能及训练稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的半监督学习方法在生成伪标签时，初始化实体嵌入往往忽略了置信度加权图的全局社区和枢纽结构，导致信息利用不充分。

Method: 1. 利用置信度加权图拉普拉斯矩阵的最小非平凡特征向量初始化实体嵌入，以融入社区和枢纽结构；2. 应用无偏小批量狄利克雷能量正则化器，以在训练早期强制保持结构一致性。

Result: 在两个UKG数据集上，QUEST在8个指标-数据集对中的6个上优于先前方法，其余2个匹配最佳结果，同时消除了稠密图上观察到的训练不稳定性峰值。

Conclusion: 结合谱结构先验与图狄利克雷能量正则化器，能有效提高UKG补全任务的准确性、训练稳定性和检查点可靠性。

Abstract: Uncertain knowledge graphs (UKGs) extend knowledge graphs by assigning each triple a continuous confidence score. Since most possible triples lack observed confidences, recent methods rely on semi-supervised learning to generate pseudo-labels. These methods initialize entity embeddings without using the confidence-weighted graph, discarding its global community and hub structure. We introduce QUEST, which adds no trainable parameters to the standard confidence-distribution learning pipeline. First, QUEST initializes entity embeddings using the smallest non-trivial eigenvectors of the confidence-weighted graph Laplacian, incorporating community and hub structure before training. Second, QUEST applies an unbiased mini-batch Dirichlet energy regularizer to enforce early-stage structural consistency. On two UKG datasets, QUEST improves confidence prediction and link prediction on six of eight metric-dataset pairs over prior methods and matches the previous best on the remaining two, while removing the instability spike observed on dense graphs. These results indicate that spectral structural priors combined with a graph Dirichlet energy regularizer improve accuracy, training stability, and checkpoint reliability in UKG completion.

</details>


### [333] [A Comparative Study of Graph Representations for GNN-Based Power Grid Control in L2RPN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02538)
*Adrian Degenkolb,Qiong Huang,Benjamin Schäfer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了深度强化学习中图构建对电网控制的影响，发现图复杂度与任务粒度的匹配比最大化表示丰富性更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图构建是深度强化学习在电网控制中的关键但未被充分研究的设计选择，需要系统比较不同图表示的效果。

Method: 在L2RPN环境中对不同图表示（包括物理拓扑、电气敏感性和混合变体）进行控制实验比较。

Result: 图复杂度与任务粒度的匹配比最大化表示丰富性更重要，大规模控制表示研究具有重要意义。

Conclusion: 强调了在电网控制任务中选择合适的图表示的重要性，并呼吁进行更多大规模的控制表示研究。

Abstract: Graph construction is a critical but underexamined design choice in deep reinforcement learning for power grid control. We present a controlled experimental comparison of different graph representations, including physical topology, electrical-sensitivity, and hybrid variants for topology control in the Learning to Run a Power Network (L2RPN) environment. Our findings indicate that matching graph complexity to task granularity is more important than maximizing representational richness, and highlight the importance of controlled representation studies at scale.

</details>


### [334] [TrajMind: Chaining Role-Specialized LoRAs for Fast-and-Slow Collective Trajectory Anomaly Diagnosis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02540)
*Jiahao Wu,Zhenqun Yang,Chen Jason Zhang,Qing Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出TrajMind框架，采用快慢双路径设计，结合视觉 - 语言模型与LoRA适配器，实现城市轨迹集体异常的高效检测与诊断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有异常检测方法要么只能提供分数或标签，缺乏丰富语义，要么视觉 - 语言管道无法兼顾可验证诊断与低延迟监控，难以从源轨迹中识别集体模式并恢复精确事件细节。

Method: 提出TrajMind快慢框架，在冻结的视觉 - 语言骨干网络上切换三个角色专用LoRA适配器。慢路径（TrajMind_slow）通过基于画布的类型划分、类型条件定位和可执行验证，生成结构化、有证据支持的诊断；快路径（TrajMind_fast）通过单次纯文本传递筛选每个窗口，提供高效结构化警报。

Result: TrajMind_slow在异常类型划分和定位上分别比最强基线高出15.3和13.8个百分点，且跨城市迁移效果稳定；TrajMind_fast延迟降低41.1%，二分类平衡准确率至少保持93.5%。

Conclusion: TrajMind框架能够跨城市提供准确、有证据支持的诊断，同时实现高效的前线监控，解决了集体异常诊断中语义丰富性与低延迟监控的平衡问题。

Abstract: Diagnosing collective anomalies from urban trajectories is increasingly important for traffic governance, as it reveals what happened, who was involved, and where and when the event occurred. Existing detectors efficiently produce scores or labels, whereas vision--language pipelines provide richer semantics; neither couples verifiable diagnosis with low-latency monitoring. The central challenge is to recognize collective patterns and recover exact event details from the source trajectories without running the full diagnostic pipeline for every monitored window. We therefore separate always-on screening from on-demand diagnosis: screening raises alerts, while diagnosis releases only source-verified what--who--where--when records. We present TrajMind, a fast-and-slow framework that switches three role-specialized LoRA adapters over one frozen vision--language backbone. Its slow path, \textit{TrajMind$_{\text{slow}}$}, chains canvas-based typing, type-conditioned localization over serialized trajectories, and executable verification, yielding structured, evidence-backed diagnoses. Additionally, the fast path, \textit{TrajMind$_{\text{fast}}$}, screens each window in a single text-only pass, delivering efficient structured alerts. Extensive experiments show that, TrajMind$_{\mathrm{slow}}$ outperforms the strongest baselines by at least $15.3$ percentage points in anomaly typing and $13.8$ percentage points in localization. These gains persist under cross-city transfer, and TrajMind$_{\mathrm{fast}}$ reduces latency by $41.1\%$ and maintains binary balanced accuracy of at least $93.5\%$. Together, TrajMind delivers accurate, evidence-backed diagnoses across cities and efficient front-line monitoring.

</details>


### [335] [Learn from Whoever Is Right: Answer-Verified Multi-Teacher Distillation for Multi-Domain LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02548)
*Xixiang He,Xingming Li,Baiqi Wu,Qiyao Sun,Xuanyu Ji,Ao Cheng,Qingyong Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出MT-SDPO方法，通过基于样本而非领域的教师可靠性验证，将多个冻结教师模型的知识蒸馏到单一学生模型中，显著提升了模型在弱项领域的表现并缩小了领域差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多教师强化学习方法通常依赖领域标签将样本路由到对应的教师，但领域专家的平均能力并不代表其在每个具体样本上都正确，而其他领域的教师有时反而能提供正确答案。因此，需要按样本识别可靠教师，而非按领域分配。

Method: 提出多教师自蒸馏策略优化（MT-SDPO），包含三个核心组件：(1) 自锚点（self-anchors），利用同组内的正确 rollout 监督当前 rollout；(2) 答案验证资格（answer-verified eligibility），仅当教师答案通过验证器时才允许其监督样本；(3) 特权蒸馏（privileged distillation），将锚点和所有验证反馈合并为上下文，供指数移动平均自教师读取，而学生模型不可见，从而保持部署时策略单一。

Result: 在三个模型家族的五个学生模型上的实验显示，MT-SDPO将Qwen3-8B最弱领域的性能提升了14.79分，并将领域差距缩小了74.7%，实现了比按领域匹配单一教师更好的平衡效果。

Conclusion: 决定教师可靠性的应是基于样本的验证结果而非领域归属，MT-SDPO通过这种机制成功统一了多领域教师能力，构建了更均衡且可部署的单一模型。

Abstract: Modern large language models (LLMs) rely on reinforcement learning to build strong capabilities in individual domains, but integrating those capabilities into a single deployable model remains challenging. By routing each sample to the teacher whose domain matches it, existing approaches let a domain label decide which teacher provides supervision. However, domain expertise holds only on average: the matched teacher is not always correct on a given sample, while a teacher from another domain sometimes is. The reliable teacher therefore has to be identified per sample, not per domain. In this paper, we introduce Multi-Teacher Self-Distillation Policy Optimization (MT-SDPO), an on-policy distillation method that unifies several frozen teachers into one student model. MT-SDPO consists of three components: (1) self-anchors, where a rollout is supervised by a correct rollout from its own group; (2) answer-verified eligibility, where a teacher may supervise a sample only if its own answer passes a verifier; and (3) privileged distillation, which merges the anchor and all verified feedback into one context that an exponential moving average self-teacher reads and the student does not, thereby keeping one policy at deployment. Across five students from three model families, MT-SDPO lifts the weakest domain of Qwen3-8B by 14.79 points and narrows its domain gap by 74.7%, a better balance than serving one matched teacher per domain. Verified reliability, not domain membership, should decide who teaches. Code is available at https://github.com/hexixiang/MT-SDPO.

</details>


### [336] [ProbeMatchDTI: Probe-Driven Multi-Scale Biochemical Pattern Matching for Drug-Target Interaction Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02549)
*Quan Hao,Mengyue Fan,Zifan Dong,Youru Li,Jianduo Zhao,Lechuan Xu,Hao Zhang,Fei Xia,Jigang Wang,Chong Qiu,Liguo Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出ProbeMatchDTI，一个基于模式探针的药物 - 靶点相互作用预测框架，通过保留弱相关生化信号和多尺度建模提升预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生化表示学习方法在DTI预测中倾向于聚合主导分子模式，抑制了如功能基团和残基上下文模式等弱但关键的结合信号，限制了多尺度生化对应关系的建模。

Method: 提出ProbeMatchDTI框架，包含IterProbe和BindingProbe两个模块。IterProbe通过可学习探针在不同细化深度选择上下文状态，保留弱生化模式并加强功能基团、局部基序和分子支架间的关联；BindingProbe在局部生化单元和整体配对层面刻画药物 - 蛋白互补性，联合建模细粒度相互作用和多尺度对应关系。

Result: 在BindingDB和DrugBank数据集上AUC-ROC分别提升2.0%和0.5%；特征级模式分析验证了探针驱动的多尺度生化模式匹配行为；并与证据引导的药物发现工作流结合，展示了在候选药物优化和验证规划中的实用性。

Conclusion: ProbeMatchDTI通过探针驱动机制有效保留弱结合相关信号并建模多尺度生化对应关系，显著提升了DTI预测性能，具有实际应用价值。

Abstract: Drug-target interaction (DTI) prediction is an important task in AI-driven drug discovery. Although recent biochemical representation learning methods have improved DTI prediction, their passive feature aggregation tends to favor dominant molecular patterns while suppressing weak yet binding-relevant signals, such as functional groups and residue-context patterns, limiting the modeling of multi-scale biochemical correspondences. To address this issue, we propose ProbeMatchDTI, a pattern-probe-driven framework comprising IterProbe and BindingProbe. IterProbe explicitly retains contextual states across refinement depths and uses learnable probes to select them at each position before cross-entity matching, thereby preserving weak biochemical patterns and strengthening associations among functional groups, local motifs, and molecular scaffolds. BindingProbe then characterizes cross-entity drug-protein complementarity at local biochemical-unit and whole-pair levels, jointly modeling fine-grained interactions and multi-scale correspondences while preserving weaker binding-relevant associations. Extensive experiments demonstrate the superiority of ProbeMatchDTI, achieving 2.0% and 0.5% higher AUC-ROC on BindingDB and DrugBank, respectively. Feature-level pattern analyses further characterize its probe-driven behavior in cross-scale biochemical pattern matching. We further connect ProbeMatchDTI predictions with an evidence-guided downstream drug-discovery workflow, demonstrating their utility for candidate refinement and validation planning. Our code is available at https://github.com/developer-hq/ProbeMatchDTI

</details>


### [337] [Online Reinforcement Learning in the Met Office Unified Model through Distributed Model-Agent Coupling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02566)
*Pritthijit Nath,Sebastian Schemm,Peter Haynes,Emily Shuckburgh,Mark Webb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将分布式强化学习（RL）代理与全球天气预报模型（UKMO UM）耦合的方法，通过在垂直模型层级上应用有界的位温修正，实现了数值稳定性并显著降低了预报误差（如热带地区Z500误差降低约45%），验证了在线学习结合非引导推理在业务系统中进行偏差校正的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习修正若要补充数值天气预报（NWP），必须能够适应不断演变的模型状态，同时保持动力学一致性和数值稳定性；现有研究缺乏在全球预报模型中验证这一能力的有效框架。

Method: 利用秩局部张量（rank-local tensors）将Met Office统一模型（UM）与分布式RL代理耦合；采用DDPG（深度确定性策略梯度）Actor网络，在70个垂直层级间共享权重，对模型倾向项施加有界的位温修正；通过10次引导训练预报（nudging training forecasts）以UKMO业务分析为反事实目标进行训练，随后在非引导预报中评估冻结策略。

Result: 耦合工作流成功完成训练并保持数值稳定；相较于匹配的本地UM预报，学习到的策略在6个纬度带中的4个显著降低了500hPa位势高度（Z500）的平均绝对误差（MAE），其中南北热带地区分别减少了45.8%和40.8%；海平面气压（MSLP）误差在3个纬度带中也有下降，0-30°N区域最大降幅达27.3%。

Conclusion: 该单案例实验证明了分布式在线学习后接非引导推理在业务系统中的巨大潜力和可行性，为在 operational 系统中应用基于RL的偏差校正和参数化方案奠定了基础。

Abstract: Machine-learnt corrections can complement numerical weather prediction only if they adapt to the evolving model state while preserving dynamical consistency and numerical stability. To test this within a global forecasting model, we couple the Met Office (UKMO) Unified Model (UM) with distributed RL agents through rank-local tensors. A DDPG actor shares weights across the 70 vertical model levels of each atmospheric column and applies bounded potential-temperature corrections to the model tendencies. Across ten nudged training forecasts, nudging calculations towards the UKMO operational analysis provides an immediate counterfactual target. The frozen policy is then evaluated in a non-nudged forecast for inference. The coupled workflow successfully completes training and remains numerically stable in the evaluated case.
